[PDF 第 1 页 | B001_010_1] EN: A B S T R A C T  
[PDF 第 1 页 | B001_010_1] 中文：摘要。

[PDF 第 1 页 | B001_011_1] EN: Artificial Intelligence (AI) techniques, especially Machine Learning (ML) and Deep Learning (DL), are increasingly used for automated defect inspection in industries like metals, ceramics, glass, and textiles. These methods process high-quality images to detect and localise defects. Inspection approaches range from manual and semi-automated (classical ML) to fully automated (DL), with DL preferred for eliminating manual feature engineering. DL models are typically supervised or unsupervised—supervised models offer high accuracy but require large labelled datasets, while unsupervised models work without labels but often yield lower performance. Emerging alternatives like semi-supervised, weakly supervised, and self-supervised learning show promising results in data-scarce settings. This paper presents a comprehensive taxonomy of DL models for defect classification, detection, and segmentation. For classification, models such as Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), transfer learning, and hybrid architectures are discussed. For segmentation tasks, encoder– decoder models, pyramid networks, and attention mechanisms are reviewed. In defect detection, the evolution of YOLO architectures is examined, particularly combinations with pyramid networks and attention modules to enhance feature extraction. Unsupervised approaches, such as Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) and diffusion models, are explored for their capacity to detect anomalies without extensive labelled datasets. Emerging strategies — including transformer-based models, few-shot learning, large language models (LLMs), and foundation models — are also covered.  
[PDF 第 1 页 | B001_011_1] 中文：人工智能（AI）技术，尤其是机器学习（ML）和深度学习（DL），正日益广泛地用于金属、陶瓷、玻璃、纺织品等行业的自动化缺陷检验。这些方法通过处理高质量图像来检测并定位缺陷。检验方法涵盖人工、半自动化（经典机器学习）以及全自动化（深度学习）；由于深度学习能够省去人工特征工程，因此更受青睐。深度学习模型通常采用有监督或无监督学习：有监督模型精度较高，但需要大量带标注的数据；无监督模型无需标签即可工作，不过性能通常较低。半监督、弱监督和自监督学习等新兴替代方法，在数据稀缺的条件下展现出良好前景。本文对用于缺陷分类、目标检测和分割的深度学习模型进行了全面分类。对于分类任务，本文讨论卷积神经网络（CNN）、迁移学习及混合架构等模型；对于分割任务，综述编码器—解码器模型、金字塔网络和注意力机制；对于缺陷目标检测，考察 YOLO 架构的演进，尤其关注通过与金字塔网络和注意力模块结合来增强特征提取能力的方案。本文也探讨生成对抗网络（GAN）和扩散模型等无监督方法，分析其在无需大量标注数据的情况下检测异常的能力。此外，还涵盖基于 Transformer 的模型、小样本学习、大语言模型（LLM）和基础模型等新兴策略。

[PDF 第 1 页 | B001_011_2] EN: Despite recent advancements, challenges remain in ensuring model reliability due to overconfidence and poor calibration. We address this by discussing model trustworthiness, human-in-the-loop frameworks, and reliability assessment techniques critical for industrial deployment. This review analyses the current strengths and limitations of existing approaches, identifies ongoing challenges, and highlights future directions in ML and DL-based defect inspection across various materials and defect types.  
[PDF 第 1 页 | B001_011_2] 中文：尽管近年取得了进展，模型的过度自信和校准不足仍给可靠性保障带来挑战。为此，本文讨论模型可信性、人在回路中的框架，以及工业部署所需的关键可靠性评估技术。本文分析现有方法的优势与局限，识别持续存在的挑战，并指出面向不同材料和缺陷类型、基于机器学习与深度学习开展缺陷检验的未来研究方向。

[PDF 第 2 页 | B002_006_1] EN: 1. Introduction  
[PDF 第 2 页 | B002_006_1] 中文：一、简介

[PDF 第 2 页 | B002_007_1] EN: During the manufacturing of products in complex industrial processes, defects such as internal holes [1], pits [2], and scratches [3] arise on the surface, edges and corners due to errors in machine design which adversely not only affects the quality of the product but also its performance and value [2,4]. Different types of defects are visible on different products (such as scratch on steel [5], blob on textile [6], crease on carbide [7] and chipping on glass [8], etc.). In addition, defects on products not only cause waste of resources, decrease revenue, and diminish customer satisfaction but also add additional cost for identification of defects [9].  
[PDF 第 2 页 | B002_007_1] 中文：在复杂的工业流程中制造产品时，由于机械设计的误差，在表面、棱角处会出现内孔[1]、凹坑[2]、划伤[3]等缺陷，不仅影响产品的质量，而且影响产品的性能和价值[2,4]。不同产品上会出现不同类型的缺陷（例如钢材上的划痕 [5]、纺织品上的斑点 [6]、碳化物上的折痕 [7] 以及玻璃上的碎裂 [8] 等）。此外，产品缺陷不仅会造成资源浪费、收入减少、客户满意度下降，还会增加识别缺陷的额外成本[9]。

[PDF 第 2 页 | B002_008_1] EN: Often new and used products are sold on the Internet (e.g., eBay, Amazon, Gumtree and Facebook marketplace). Buyers must rely on the description of the seller given with the product photos. They may have a lack of confidence to decide whether to buy a product of interest. An independent assessment system can be developed, and the inspection report can be attached to the product advertisement to increase buyer’s confidence and satisfaction. So, defect detection and classification are an important topic that is treated as a specific problem to the industry at the product manufacturing and production phases [10]. Manual defect identification requires experts to inspect and handle products  
[PDF 第 2 页 | B002_008_1] 中文：新产品和二手产品通常在互联网上销售（例如 eBay、亚马逊、Gumtree 和 Facebook 市场）。买家必须依赖卖家对产品照片的描述。他们可能缺乏信心来决定是否购买感兴趣的产品。可以开发独立的评估系统，并将检验报告附在产品广告上，以增加买家的信心和满意度。因此，缺陷检测和分类是一个重要的课题，在产品制造和生产阶段被视为行业的特定问题[10]。人工缺陷识别需要专家对产品进行检查和处理

[PDF 第 2 页 | B002_009_1] EN: physically. Thus, this is not only expensive but also makes the inspection result subjective. Moreover, this poses products to be more defective due to physical handling. For example, Mital et al. [11] uses a physical inspection method for product parts in the industrial sector. It works excellently in some cases but has performance consequences because of poor detection and lower real-time performance [9]. Thus, this method does not satisfy the requirement of quality inspection of modern industrial production [12].  
[PDF 第 2 页 | B002_009_1] 中文：身体上。因此，这不仅成本昂贵，而且使检查结果变得主观。此外，由于物理处理，这导致产品存在更多缺陷。例如，米塔尔等人。 [11]对工业部门的产品零件采用物理检查方法。它在某些情况下工作得很好，但由于检测不佳和实时性能较低而产生性能影响[9]。因此，该方法不能满足现代工业生产质量检验的要求[12]。

[PDF 第 2 页 | B002_010_1] EN: Therefore, a more reliable and efficient methodology should be introduced. Automated defect detection methodology has more advantages over manual identification. It saves time, decreases the overall production cost, eliminates physical product handling, increases product quality, and works in the long run with high efficiency. For example, machine vision techniques can take images of products and allow computers to inspect the images. The use of computers for analysing images and the application of AI for decision-making make the process more automated and reliable [13,14]. Recently, automated methods have been investigated on various products, e.g., pharmaceutical [15], steel [5] and textile [6]. They mainly use computer vision and image processing techniques in surface defect detection [16–18]. Thus, modern manufacturing and industrial processes have been integrated with image processing, computer vision and AI-based systems [12,19, 20] to improve the accuracy of detection and to reduce manpower requirements.  
[PDF 第 2 页 | B002_010_1] 中文：因此，应该引入更可靠、更有效的方法。自动缺陷检测方法比手动识别具有更多优势。它节省了时间，降低了总体生产成本，消除了物理产品处理，提高了产品质量，并且从长远来看，效率很高。例如，机器视觉技术可以拍摄产品图像并允许计算机检查图像。使用计算机分析图像以及应用人工智能进行决策使过程更加自动化和可靠[13,14]。最近，自动化方法已在各种产品上进行了研究，例如药品[15]、钢铁[5]和纺织品[6]。他们主要使用计算机视觉和图像处理技术进行表面缺陷检测[16-18]。因此，现代制造和工业流程已与图像处理、计算机视觉和基于人工智能的系统集成[12,19,20]，以提高检测的准确性并减少人力需求。

## PDF 第 3 页

[查看原 PDF 第 3 页](<AI-enabled defect detection in industrial products_ A comprehensive survey, key insights and future research challenges.pdf#page=3>)

[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_001_1] EN: Fig. 1. A taxonomy of product defects and their detection techniques.  
[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_001_1] 中文：图 1. 产品缺陷分类及其检测技术。

[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_002_1] EN: Semi-automated defect detection methods, based on classical image processing and machine learning (ML) techniques, rely heavily on manual parameter tuning and feature engineering. Although the application of classical ML techniques (e.g., Support Vector Machine) on the features (e.g., mean, variance, curvature) extracted using image processing techniques (e.g., texture analysis) improves the defect detection performance, the performance is still under the required satisfaction level. In contrast, deep learning (DL) models [21] eliminate manual feature extraction by learning directly from images, enabling full automation, improved performance, and wider adoption in industrial defect classification and localisation.  
[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_002_1] 中文：基于经典图像处理和机器学习（ML）技术的半自动缺陷检测方法严重依赖手动参数调整和特征工程。尽管在使用图像处理技术（例如纹理分析）提取的特征（例如均值、方差、曲率）上应用经典机器学习技术（例如支持向量机）提高了缺陷检测性能，但性能仍然低于所需的满意度水平。相比之下，深度学习（DL）模型[21]通过直接从图像中学习来消除手动特征提取，从而实现完全自动化、提高性能并在工业缺陷分类和定位中得到更广泛的采用。

[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_003_1] EN: Furthermore, supervised DL models require large labelled datasets for high accuracy [22], but data collection is labour-intensive. To mitigate this, researchers use data augmentation [23] and synthesis algorithms to address dataset imbalance [24]. Despite these efforts, labelling remains time-consuming. Segmentation helps locate defects [25], yet pixel-level annotation adds cost. As an alternative, unsupervised DL uses unlabelled data, though image scarcity persists in some domains. Dong et al. [26] shows promising results by combining data augmentation with unsupervised segmentation on fewer unlabelled images.  
[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_003_1] 中文：此外，有监督的深度学习模型需要大量标记数据集才能实现高精度[22]，但数据收集是劳动密集型的。为了缓解这一问题，研究人员使用数据增强 [23] 和合成算法来解决数据集不平衡问题 [24]。尽管做出了这些努力，贴标签仍然很耗时。分割有助于定位缺陷[25]，但像素级标注会增加成本。作为替代方案，无监督深度学习使用未标记的数据，尽管图像稀缺在某些领域仍然存在。董等人。 [26]通过将数据增强与对较少未标记图像的无监督分割相结合，显示出有希望的结果。

[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_004_1] EN: Existing surveys on defect detection [27–33] primarily focus on specific products and predominantly discuss the application of DL techniques. However, these surveys lack in detail descriptions regarding the quantification of defect size, determining the location of defects, and the utilisation of image-capturing devices based on defect size. However, these aspects are important for developing comprehensive defect detection methodologies and improving the accuracy and efficiency of defect analysis in various industries. This study focuses on the state of development of defect detection on industrial products utilising machine vision. Fig. 1 illustrates the overview of this work such as image processing, ML and DL methods, which are discussed and critically analysed in detail, for defect detection.  
[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_004_1] 中文：现有的缺陷检测调查[27-33]主要关注特定产品，并主要讨论深度学习技术的应用。然而，这些调查缺乏关于缺陷尺寸的量化、缺陷位置的确定以及基于缺陷尺寸的图像捕捉设备的利用的详细描述。然而，这些方面对于开发全面的缺陷检测方法和提高各行业缺陷分析的准确性和效率非常重要。本研究重点关注利用机器视觉进行工业产品缺陷检测的发展状况。图 1 展示了这项工作的概述，例如图像处理、ML 和 DL 方法，这些方法都被详细讨论和批判性地分析，用于缺陷检测。

[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_005_1] EN: The key contributions of this paper are listed as follows:  
[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_005_1] 中文：本文的主要贡献如下：

[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_006_1] EN: • This study examines the characteristics of different defect types found on industrial products. • Provides a comprehensive overview of semi-automated and automated methods that are applied to detect defects on industrial products. • Categorises DL-based automated models that have been employed for defect detection applications. • Highlights the challenges and summarises the potential research directions for quality assessment of industrial products.  
[PDF 第 3 页 | B003_006_1] 中文：• 这项研究调查了工业产品上发现的不同缺陷类型的特征。 • 全面概述用于检测工业产品缺陷的半自动化和自动化方法。 • 对已用于缺陷检测应用的基于深度学习的自动化模型进行分类。 • 强调工业产品质量评估面临的挑战并总结潜在的研究方向。

Initially, articles are searched using the full text of the papers based on specific objects (e.g., steel, wood, glass, fabric etc.) and key terms. Duplicate documents and non English article are eliminated. Following this, some articles are removed after reading the abstracts which is irrelevant to the research. Finally, around

[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_005_1] EN: Semi-automated methods work well for regular patterns but struggle with micro defects (e.g., cracks on metal). In contrast, machine vision offers more accurate detection and classification. However, the variability in defect shapes and sizes makes it difficult to classify all defects and build a comprehensive database. Fig. 3 shows examples of defects across different products. Henceforth, this section represents different categories of defects based on materials. The following subsections describe defect types, their characteristics, inspection methods and imaging technologies.  
[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_005_1] 中文：半自动方法对常规模式运作良好,但与微缺陷(如金属裂缝)抗争,而机器视觉则提供更准确的检测和分类,然而,缺陷形状和大小的变化使得难以对所有缺陷进行分类并建立一个综合数据库。图3举例说明了不同产品之间的缺陷。因此,本节代表基于材料的不同类别的缺陷。以下各小节描述了缺陷类型、特征、检查方法和成像技术。

[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_006_1] EN: 2.1. Defect categories and materials  
[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_006_1] 中文：2.1. 缺陷类别和材料

[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_007_1] EN: This study focuses on several industrial products, made of metal, ceramic, textile, paper, wood and carbide, which may have various types of defects. Some defects are specific to specific products, for example, cracks can happen on products made of ceramic [35,36,48], metal or glass [8,45], but they do not happen on products such as art paper, fabric and trading card [38–40,49,50], which may have other specific defects like fray, colouring and printing. Printed products such as trading cards and art paper can have two types of defects. First, an art paper may have defects such as dents and fray on its edges and these defects cause damage directly to the product material (e.g., paper). Second, defects like pattern error and colour imperfection [47] are related to the printing quality of the art paper and they can happen during the painting or printing as each printing device has its own  
[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_007_1] 中文：这项研究侧重于由金属、陶瓷、纺织品、纸张、木料和碳化物组成的若干工业产品,这些产品可能存在各种缺陷。 有些缺陷是特定产品的特有缺陷,例如陶瓷[35,36,48]、金属或玻璃[8,45]制成的产品可能会发生裂缝,但艺术纸、织物和交易卡[38-40,49,50]等产品却不会发生裂缝,因为这些产品可能有其他具体的缺陷,如毛线、彩色和印刷。 印刷产品,如交易卡和艺术品纸,可能有两种缺陷。 首先,艺术纸张的边缘可能有凹痕和壁画等缺陷,这些缺陷直接对产品材料造成损害(例如纸张)。 其次,图案错误和彩色不完善等缺陷[47]与艺术品的印刷质量有关,它们可以在绘画或印刷期间发生,因为每个印刷设备都有自己的作品。

[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_008_1] EN: unique printing limitations. In contrast, products such as tiles and glass have only the first type of defects (e.g., cracks and pinholes in tiles). Therefore, all possible defects in products are categorised into two: printing and non-printing. Table 1 represents various defects in these two categories along with the materials where the defects can happen. Printing defects like pattern errors, colour imperfection and poor line quality are visible on printed products (e.g., Pokemon cards, clothes, art papers). Non-printing defects are found in other industrial products, for example, steel, glass, and metal.  
[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_008_1] 中文：独特的印刷限制。相比之下，瓷砖和玻璃等产品仅具有第一类缺陷（例如瓷砖中的裂纹和针孔）。因此，所有可能出现的产品缺陷分为两类：印刷缺陷和非印刷缺陷。表 1 列出了这两类中的各种缺陷以及可能发生缺陷的材料。印刷产品（例如口袋妖怪卡片、衣服、艺术纸）上可以看到图案错误、颜色缺陷和线条质量差等印刷缺陷。非印刷缺陷也存在于其他工业产品中，例如钢铁、玻璃和金属。

[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_009_1] EN: 2.2. Defect characteristics  
[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_009_1] 中文：2.2.缺陷特征

[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_010_1] EN: Defects can be of different shapes and provide different levels of human perceptions. Table 2 shows that defects are usually visible on three product locations: surface, edges, and corners. While defects on a product surface may have regular shapes (e.g., line, pattern), defects on product edges [8] and corners [35,36] often have random shapes. All defects (e.g., cracks, creases, colour imperfections) are visible to human eyes as this study focuses on the visibility and size of defects while estimating product quality. Some of the defects (e.g., cracks, creases) are palpable as they are perceptible with a finger touch.  
[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_010_1] 中文：缺陷可以具有不同的形状并提供不同程度的人类感知。表 2 显示缺陷通常在三个产品位置可见：表面、边缘和角落。虽然产品表面上的缺陷可能具有规则形状（例如线条、图案），但产品边缘 [8] 和拐角 [35,36] 上的缺陷通常具有随机形状。所有缺陷（例如裂纹、折痕、颜色缺陷）都是人眼可见的，因为本研究在评估产品质量时重点关注缺陷的可见性和大小。有些缺陷（例如裂纹、折痕）是明显的，因为用手指触摸即可察觉。

[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_011_1] EN: Defects can happen for various reasons on aforementioned product locations. For example, cracks [7] may happen on edges and corners due to the movement of the product, while they occur on the surface during production due to high pressure on the product. Some defects (e.g., corrosion, contamination) may happen on the surface due to unfavourable weather conditions or chemical leakage. Poor construction of a product during manufacturing or assembling may be liable for these defects on the product surface when the product is in use.  
[PDF 第 4 页 | B004_011_1] 中文：上述产品位置可能因各种原因而发生缺陷。例如，由于产品的移动，边缘和角落可能会出现裂纹[7]，而在生产过程中，由于产品承受的高压，裂纹可能会出现在表面。由于不利的天气条件或化学品泄漏，表面可能会出现一些缺陷（例如腐蚀、污染）。产品在制造或组装过程中的不良结构可能会在产品使用时导致产品表面出现这些缺陷。

[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_083_1] EN: 2.3. Defects inspection  
[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_083_1] 中文：2.3.缺陷检查

[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_084_1] EN: Defects inspection plays an important role as it involves checking the various attributes of a product and testing it to ensure that it meets specified standards. Defect inspection can take place in two levels, during manufacturing [8,43,44] and when the product is inuse [3,4,43,44,51–54]. Defects, which occur during the manufacturing of a product, can be inspected when the quality assessment of a product can be done by the company before bringing the product to the market. So, defect detection during manufacturing level is conducted on new products. When a product is in-use defect inspection can be conducted to estimate the status of the product. For example, one can buy a new Pokémon card from a company and use this card for some time before he/she sells this card. So, it is necessary to assess the quality of the card to estimate its current value. Table 3 represents different types of products and defects with their inspection levels.  
[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_084_1] 中文：缺陷检验起着重要作用，因为它涉及检查产品的各种属性并对其进行测试以确保其符合指定的标准。缺陷检查可以在制造过程中[8,43,44]和产品使用时[3,4,43,44,51–54]两个层面进行。当公司在将产品推向市场之前对产品进行质量评估时，可以检查产品制造过程中出现的缺陷。因此，制造阶段的缺陷检测是对新产品进行的。当产品在使用中时，可以进行缺陷检查来估计产品的状态。例如，一个人可以从一家公司购买一张新的神奇宝贝卡，并在出售该卡之前使用该卡一段时间。因此，有必要评估卡片的质量来估算其当前的价值。表3列出了不同类型的产品和缺陷及其检验级别。

[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_085_1] EN: Another attribute that has been estimated in both manufacturing and in-use levels is defect estimation (e.g., size, quantity). To check the product quality more deeply it needs to measure the quantity and length of the defect size. For example, the quality of the products made of wood has been assessed through some measurements such as length, quantity, and depth of defects on products while for other products, such as fabric, quality assessment has been done by length and quantity of the defects on products.  
[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_085_1] 中文：在制造和使用级别中估计的另一个属性是缺陷估计（例如尺寸、数量）。为了更深入地检查产品质量，需要测量缺陷尺寸的数量和长度。例如，木材产品的质量通过产品缺陷的长度、数量和深度等一些测量来评估，而对于其他产品，例如织物，则通过产品缺陷的长度和数量来评估质量。

[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_086_1] EN: 2.4. Defects imaging  
[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_086_1] 中文：2.4.缺陷成像

[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_087_1] EN: Capturing the important product features and eliminating unexpected features (e.g., due to shadowing or improper lighting environment) are essential to maintaining the image quality that plays an important role in defect detection. To obtain the high-quality image of the product an appropriate image acquisition hardware (e.g., camera, scanner) with a good optical illumination platform is required. A suitable optical illumination platform overcomes the interference of environmental lighting, ensures the stability of the imaging system, and obtains an image with a high contrast.  
[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_087_1] 中文：捕获重要的产品特征并消除意外特征（例如，由于阴影或不适当的照明环境）对于保持在缺陷检测中发挥重要作用的图像质量至关重要。为了获得产品的高质量图像，需要具有良好光学照明平台的适当图像采集硬件（例如相机、扫描仪）。合适的光学照明平台可以克服环境照明的干扰，保证成像系统的稳定性，获得高对比度的图像。

[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_088_1] EN: Image acquisition hardware is chosen based on the product characteristics, defect types and sizes (e.g., small, very small) as well as the device characteristics (e.g., magnification, resolution). Table 4 represents descriptions of different devices along with their characteristics. High resolution and magnification allow for capturing more detailed information from the product that leads to an investigation of tiny defects which may not be visible to the naked eye. Thus, better performance in the process of detecting and classifying defects in general can be obtained using high-resolution images but they consume high computational resources.  
[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_088_1] 中文：根据产品特性、缺陷类型和尺寸（例如，小、非常小）以及设备特性（例如，放大率、分辨率）来选择图像采集硬件。表 4 描述了不同设备及其特性。高分辨率和放大倍率允许从产品中捕获更详细的信息，从而对肉眼可能不可见的微小缺陷进行调查。因此，使用高分辨率图像通常可以在缺陷检测和分类过程中获得更好的性能，但它们会消耗大量计算资源。

[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_089_1] EN: Among the different types of image capturing devices [55–59] that have been presented in the literature, for example, on products made of semiconductor, if the defect size is very small (e.g., 1 to 10 nm) then a scanning electron microscope (SEM) [55] is used. If the defect  
[PDF 第 5 页 | B005_089_1] 中文：在文献中提出的不同类型的图像捕获设备[55-59]中，例如，在半导体制成的产品上，如果缺陷尺寸非常小（例如1至10 nm），则使用扫描电子显微镜（SEM）[55]。如果有缺陷

[PDF 第 6 页 | B006_050_1] EN: A brief description of each image capturing device is given below.  
[PDF 第 6 页 | B006_050_1] 中文：下文简要介绍每个图像捕捉设备。

[PDF 第 6 页 | B006_051_1] EN: • Scanning Electron Microscope: An SEM [55–59] is used for defect detection on products made of semiconductors. It offers very high magnification, up to 3 million times, which allows very small, usually invisible defects, to be detected and analysed. Therefore, it provides high accuracy (e.g., 90% in [55]) when the captured data are processed with modern AI-based algorithms. 
• Digital Microscopes: A digital light microscope [60] offering a magnification range of 34 to 1,100 times can capture images of various products while effectively detecting defects of different sizes, including those that are very small or small. For example, Rolland et al. [60] used a digital microscope to capture images of cotton leaves in their work to score the leaf hairiness which achieved 86%–96% accuracy. Bukieda et al. [8] also used digital light microscopy images to inspect the quality of glasses. They also described the importance of microscopic images to detect defects in edges and surfaces.
• Digital Cameras: A digital camera is used for all materials with small, medium and large defects. The usual photosensitive devices used in industrial cameras are charged coupled devices (CCD) or complementary metal oxide semiconductor (CMOS) chips. Hsu and Lin [45] uses two CCD cameras to capture images for glass quality inspection, while Nigam and Singh [62] used one CMOS camera to capture images for crack detection in beams. 
• Photo-metric Stereo: To obtain images using photo-metric stereo [51], an object is sequentially illuminated by different light sources from different directions. It provides all information such  
[PDF 第 6 页 | B006_051_1] 中文：• 扫描电子显微镜：扫描电镜（SEM）[55–59] 用于检测半导体产品中的缺陷。它具有极高的放大倍数，最高可达300万倍，能够发现并分析通常肉眼不可见的微小缺陷。因此，当采集的数据通过现代基于人工智能的算法进行处理时，可实现很高的检测精度（例如在[55]中达到90%）。  
• 数码显微镜：一种数码光学显微镜[60]，其放大倍数范围为34至1100倍，可对多种产品进行成像，并有效检测不同尺寸的缺陷，包括非常微小或细小的缺陷。例如，Rolland等人[60]在其研究中使用数码显微镜拍摄棉花叶片图像，以评估叶片绒毛程度，准确率达到86%至96%。Bukieda等人[8]也利用数码光学显微镜图像来检测玻璃质量，并强调了显微图像在识别边缘和表面缺陷方面的重要性。  
• 数码相机：数码相机可用于检测各种材料中大小不一的缺陷。工业相机中常用的感光器件是电荷耦合器件（CCD）或互补金属氧化物半导体（CMOS）芯片。Hsu 和 Lin [45] 使用了两台 CCD 相机来采集玻璃质量检测的图像，而 Nigam 和 Singh [62] 则使用了一台 CMOS 相机来采集梁体裂纹检测的图像。  
• 光度立体法：采用光度立体法 [51] 获取图像时，会从不同方向依次用不同的光源照射物体，从而提供所有相关信息，例如

[PDF 第 6 页 | B006_052_1] EN: as contour lines as well as curvature on the surface, colour, spectral data and signal variation in horizontal and vertical directions. This approach is proficient in detecting defects of varying sizes, encompassing small, medium, and large, and possesses the unique capability to effectively identify deep defects as well. It can detect parts with diameters ranging from 65 mm to 110 mm and can detect breakage, bumps, and cracks. Saiz et al. [51] proposed a segmentation network to detect defects on metallic components using photo-metric stereo images which achieves 83% accuracy.  
[PDF 第 6 页 | B006_052_1] 中文：如轮廓线以及表面曲率、颜色、光谱数据以及水平和垂直方向的信号变化。该方法能够熟练地检测不同尺寸（包括小型、中型和大型）的缺陷，并且还具有有效识别深层缺陷的独特能力。它可以检测直径范围从65毫米到110毫米的零件，并可以检测破损、凹凸和裂纹。赛兹等人。 [51]提出了一种分割网络，使用光度立体图像检测金属部件上的缺陷，准确率达到 83%。

[PDF 第 6 页 | B006_053_1] EN: 3. Classification of defect detection techniques  
[PDF 第 6 页 | B006_053_1] 中文：3、缺陷检测技术的分类

[PDF 第 6 页 | B006_054_1] EN: The literature covering defect inspection methods can be broadly divided into three parts: (i) manual, (ii) classical image processing and ML-based semi-automated and (iii) DL-based automated. For each defect inspection method, the advantages and disadvantages of visual inspection methods are presented in Table 5. Furthermore, semi-automated and automated inspection methods are discussed in the following subsections.  
[PDF 第 6 页 | B006_054_1] 中文：涉及缺陷检测方法的文献大致可分为三个部分：(i) 手动、(ii) 经典图像处理和基于 ML 的半自动化以及 (iii) 基于 DL 的自动化。对于每种缺陷检查方法，目视检查方法的优点和缺点如表 5 所示。此外，半自动和自动检查方法将在以下小节中讨论。

The choice of a method for defect detection depends on expert opinions, success rates, and dataset availability [49,50,68– 70]. For instance, learning-based methods are effective for detecting defects in patterned surfaces, while statistical or model-based techniques

[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_016_1] EN: 3.2. DL-based automated defect detection techniques  
[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_016_1] 中文：3.2.基于深度学习的自动缺陷检测技术

[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_017_1] EN: As shown in Fig. 1, DL-based defect detection is divided into two: classification and localisation. Classification models are trained either from scratch or via transfer learning using pre-trained weights (e.g., ImageNet) to reduce computational cost [71]. Localisation approaches vary by defect type and include encoder–decoder, pyramid, attention, and YOLO-based networks.  
[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_017_1] 中文：如图1所示，基于深度学习的缺陷检测分为两部分：分类和定位。分类模型可以从头开始训练，也可以使用预先训练的权重（例如 ImageNet）通过迁移学习进行训练，以减少计算成本 [71]。定位方法因缺陷类型而异，包括编码器-解码器、金字塔、注意力和基于 YOLO 的网络。

[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_018_1] EN: Henceforth, based on dataset availability and learning strategy, DL methods are classified into two paradigms: supervised learning and unsupervised learning. Both learning methods have been used for defect classification and localisation in many industries. In addition, semi-supervised, self-supervised, and weakly supervised learning have gained attention for reducing annotation effort while maintaining competitive performance. The basic definition about the model how they work are given below:  
[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_018_1] 中文：此后，根据数据集可用性和学习策略，深度学习方法分为两种范式：监督学习和无监督学习。这两种学习方法已被用于许多行业的缺陷分类和定位。此外，半监督、自监督和弱监督学习因在保持竞争表现的同时减少标注工作而受到关注。关于模型如何工作的基本定义如下：

[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_019_1] EN: • Supervised Learning: Supervised learning has been the most widely used model with CNN for defect classification and localisation. Given a large training dataset, the supervised based models can attain substantial defect detection accuracy. However, collecting such a large labelled training dataset requires enormous manpower and makes the model expensive. This challenge can be addressed by unsupervised learning model. 
• Unsupervised Learning: Unsupervised learning addresses the challenge of defect detection without labelled data, lowering annotation costs. Common approaches include autoencoders (AEs) and generative adversarial networks (GANs). AEs learn compact latent representations by reconstructing input images, while GANs generate realistic samples through adversarial training between generator and discriminator networks. Recently, diffusion models also have gained attention for their ability to iteratively denoise random noise into high-fidelity defect-free or synthetic images, offering superior reconstruction and anomaly localisation in complex textures. 
• Semi, Self, and Weakly Supervised Learning: Semi-supervised learning (SSL) bridges the gap between supervised and unsupervised methods by leveraging a small amount of labelled data with a larger pool of unlabelled samples, offering good generalisation with reduced annotation cost. Self-supervised learning further minimises reliance on labels by learning from pretext task, such as contrastive learning showing strong results on small or imbalanced datasets.  
[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_019_1] 中文：• 监督学习：监督学习是CNN 缺陷分类和定位中使用最广泛的模型。给定大型训练数据集，基于监督的模型可以获得相当大的缺陷检测精度。然而，收集如此大的标记训练数据集需要大量的人力并且使得模型昂贵。这一挑战可以通过无监督学习模型来解决。
• 无监督学习：无监督学习解决了无需标记数据的缺陷检测的挑战，从而降低了标注成本。常见的方法包括自编码器（AE）和生成对抗网络（GAN）。 AE 通过重建输入图像来学习紧凑的潜在表示，而 GAN 通过生成器和鉴别器网络之间的对抗训练来生成真实的样本。最近，扩散模型还因其能够迭代地将随机噪声去噪为高保真无缺陷或合成图像的能力而受到关注，从而在复杂纹理中提供卓越的重建和异常定位。
• 半监督学习、自监督学习和弱监督学习：半监督学习 (SSL) 通过利用少量标记数据和大量未标记样本，弥合了监督方法和无监督方法之间的差距，提供了良好的泛化能力，同时降低了标注成本。自监督学习通过从借口任务中学习，进一步减少对标签的依赖，例如对比学习在小型或不平衡数据集上显示出强大的结果。

[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_019_2] EN: Weakly supervised learning uses coarse labels (e.g., image-level or sparse annotations) to reduce labelling effort while maintaining segmentation accuracy, though it may suffer  from lower precision and generalisability without attention or augmentation strategies.
[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_019_2] 中文：弱监督学习利用粗粒度标签（例如图像级或稀疏标注）来减少标注工作量，同时保持分割精度，但若不采用注意力机制或数据增强策略，可能会导致精度和泛化能力下降。

[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_020_1] EN: Fig. 5. Basic structure of a CNN network.  
[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_020_1] 中文：图 5.CNN 网络的基本结构。

[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_021_1] EN:  • CNN-based Networks: As shown in Fig. 5, CNNs consist of multiple layers designed to extract high-level features from input images. Convolutional layers use filters to generate feature maps, and the network is trained using backpropagation to minimise a loss function. The final output layer performs classification at the image or pixel level [72,73].
• Encoder–decoder Networks: The encoder–decoder model is an established deep learning architecture utilised for defect segmentation. The input data is mapped to a lower-dimensional latent representation using the encoder, while the decoder takes this encoded form and generates the desired output. For example, U-Net model is a notable example of encoder–decoder models [74].
• Pyramid Networks: In defect detection for multi-scale analysis pyramid networks have been utilised. The concept of this approach involves extracting features from different resolutions, enabling a more comprehensive understanding of the image across various scales. The model can effectively learn by incorporating detailed information from both low and high-resolution features [75]. 
• Attention-based Networks: Recently attention mechanisms have gained significant attention in the field of computer vision. It enhances deep learning models by selectively focusing on important input elements, improving prediction accuracy and computational efficiency [76–79]. 
[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_021_1] 中文：- **基于卷积神经网络（CNN）的模型**：如图 5 所示，卷积神经网络由多层网络结构组成，用于从输入图像中提取高层特征。卷积层通过卷积核生成特征图，网络借助反向传播算法完成训练，以最小化损失函数。最终输出层可实现图像级或像素级的分类任务 [72,73]。
- **编码器 - 解码器网络**：编码器 - 解码器是缺陷分割任务中经典的深度学习架构。编码器将输入数据映射为低维的潜在特征表示，解码器则基于该编码特征生成目标输出结果。U-Net 就是编码器 - 解码器架构的典型代表 [74]。
- **金字塔网络**：金字塔网络被应用于缺陷检测的多尺度分析场景。该方法的核心原理是从不同分辨率下提取特征，实现多尺度下对图像的全面理解；通过融合高、低分辨率特征中的细节信息，模型可完成更高效的特征学习 [75]。
- **基于注意力机制的网络**：近年来，注意力机制在计算机视觉领域得到了广泛关注。它通过让模型选择性聚焦输入中的关键信息来优化深度学习模型，同时提升预测精度与计算效率 [76–79]。
  
[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_021_2] EN: • YOLO-based Networks: Defect detection networks are divided into two types: one-stage and two-stage algorithms.  One-stage models are YOLO [80] and SSD [81], predict object locations and categories in a single step and offers faster detection. Two-stage models, such as Faster R-CNN [82], first generate object proposals and then refine detection. Recently YOLO model and its several series gains more popularity in the defect detection. 
• Other Networks: Beyond these networks, recent advanced networks include transformer-based models, foundation models, and large language models (LLMs) also gain more popularity. Transformers excel in capturing long-range dependencies and are effective for both classification and segmentation tasks. Foundation models, such as SAM and CLIP, offer strong generalisation with minimal supervision, while few-shot learning enables effective training on limited data. LLMs, when combined with vision models, enhance explainability and support multi-modal defect analysis through language-driven insights.  
[PDF 第 7 页 | B007_021_2] 中文：- **基于 YOLO 的网络**：缺陷检测网络可分为一阶段与两阶段两类算法。YOLO [80]、SSD [81] 属于一阶段模型，可单次前向传播同时完成目标定位与类别预测，检测速度更快；以 Faster R-CNN [82] 为代表的两阶段模型，则先生成目标候选框，再对检测结果进行精修。近年来，YOLO 模型及其各代衍生系列在缺陷检测领域的应用愈发广泛。
- **其他网络**：除上述网络外，基于 Transformer 的模型、基础模型以及大语言模型（LLM）等前沿网络也受到广泛关注。Transformer 擅长捕捉长程依赖关系，在分类与分割任务中均表现优异；SAM、CLIP 等基础模型仅需少量监督即可具备强泛化能力，搭配小样本学习技术可在有限数据上实现高效训练。大语言模型与视觉模型结合后，可提升模型可解释性，并借助语言驱动的语义洞察支撑多模态缺陷分析。

## PDF 第 8 页

[查看原 PDF 第 8 页](<AI-enabled defect detection in industrial products_ A comprehensive survey, key insights and future research challenges.pdf#page=8>)

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_001_1] EN: Fig. 6. Architecture of an automated defect detection system.  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_001_1] 中文：图6. 自动缺陷检测系统的结构。

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_002_1] EN: Products move on a conveyor belt under suitable  lighting, with industrial camera sets capturing image data from various angles and storing it on the industrial computer. Data acquisition depends on specific application requirements such as resolution, speed, and accuracy, as well as factors like lighting conditions and object size. The description of defect image-capturing devices for different types of products is provided in Section 2.4.  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_002_1] 中文：产品在适宜光照条件下沿传送带传送，由多组工业相机从不同角度采集图像数据，并存储至工控机中。数据采集方案需结合分辨率、采集速率、精度要求等具体应用需求，同时兼顾光照条件、目标尺寸等因素确定。第 2.4 节将针对不同品类产品的缺陷图像采集设备展开详细说明。

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_003_1] EN: Common types of cameras for data acquisition include area scan cameras for 2D area imaging and 3D cameras for depth information for real-time applications. Moreover, scanners can also be utilised in certain applications where consistent lighting is crucial for defect detection. For instance, trading cards [83] can be scanned to detect defects while consistent lighting provided by scanners ensures uniform image quality, aiding in accurate defect detection. The visual system processing occurs on the computer, where modern machine vision models analyse the captured image to determine product defects. If a defect is detected, the system removes the product from the conveyor belt; otherwise, the product proceeds forward.  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_003_1] 中文：用于数据采集的常见类型相机包括用于 2D 区域成像的区域扫描相机和用于实时应用深度信息的 3D 相机。此外，扫描仪还可用于某些应用，其中一致的照明对于缺陷检测至关重要。例如，可以扫描交易卡[83]来检测缺陷，而扫描仪提供的一致照明可确保均匀的图像质量，有助于准确的缺陷检测。视觉系统处理发生在计算机上，现代机器视觉模型分析捕获的图像以确定产品缺陷。如果检测到缺陷，系统会将产品从传送带上移除；否则，产品继续前进。

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_004_1] EN: 4. Efficacy of defect detection techniques using semi-automated  learning  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_004_1] 中文：4. 使用半监督学习缺陷检测技术的功效

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_006_1] EN: Machine vision is a popular approach for quality inspection of industrial products which utilises advanced computer vision algorithms and imaging technology to perform efficient inspections with reduced or without any manual assistance. Many studies have explored this approach, encompassing image processing and ML methods across various product domains. This section presents a comprehensive overview of these studies in two key aspects: image processing and machine learning.  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_006_1] 中文：机器视觉是一种流行的工业产品质量检测方法，它利用先进的计算机视觉算法和成像技术来执行高效的检测，减少或无需任何手动辅助。许多研究都探索了这种方法，涵盖各个产品领域的图像处理和机器学习方法。本节从两个关键方面全面概述这些研究：图像处理和机器学习。

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_007_1] EN: 4.1. Image processing-based semi-automated techniques  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_007_1] 中文：4.1.基于图像处理的半自动化技术

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_008_1] EN: Image processing techniques [21,84,85] detect defects by identifying deviations from normal surface patterns, particularly when defects are visually distinct from the background. Common methods include edge-based, wavelet-based, and feature-based approaches [86, 87]. Edge detection techniques, such as Canny and Otsu are widely used; for instance, Parenti et al. [7] applies Canny edge for carbide inspection, detecting defects as small as 10–50 μm, while Bukieda et al. [8] uses Otsu thresholding to identify cracks and dark spots on glass with 80% and 60% accuracy, respectively. Wavelet-based methods enable multi-scale analysis. Lu and Chen [88] achieves 99% accuracy in detecting cracks on metal surfaces using wavelet-based segmentation, and Nigam and Singh [62] combines wavelet analysis with slope detection for beam crack identification. Feature-based techniques such as local binary pattern (LBP) combines with extreme learning machines (ELM) have shown 95% accuracy in cold-rolled steel strip defect detection [89], while colour and texture-based grading methods applies to ceramic tiles have achieved up to 96% accuracy [90,91].  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_008_1] 中文：图像处理技术[21,84,85]通过识别与正常表面图案的偏差来检测缺陷，特别是当缺陷在视觉上与背景不同时。常见的方法包括基于边缘、基于小波和基于特征的方法[86, 87]。边缘检测技术，如Canny、Otsu等被广泛应用；例如，Parenti 等人。 [7] 应用 Canny 边缘进行硬质合金检查，检测小至 10-50 μm 的缺陷，而 Bukieda 等人。 [8] 使用 Otsu 阈值来识别玻璃上的裂纹和黑点，准确率分别为 80% 和 60%。基于小波的方法可以实现多尺度分析。 Lu 和 Chen [88] 使用基于小波的分割检测金属表面裂纹的准确率达到 99%，Nigam 和 Singh [62] 将小波分析与斜率检测结合起来进行梁裂纹识别。基于特征的技术，如局部二值模式 (LBP) 与极限学习机 (ELM) 相结合，在冷轧钢带缺陷检测中显示出 95% 的准确率 [89]，而适用于瓷砖的基于颜色和纹理的分级方法已实现高达 96% 的准确率 [90,91]。

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_009_1] EN: Table 6 summarises the performance of these techniques, including  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_009_1] 中文：表 6 总结了这些技术的性能，包括

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_010_1] EN: device types and accuracy metrics. For instance, Singh and Yadav [35] reports 96% accuracy using image processing and mathematical operations on tile images, and Matić et al. [48] reaches 90% accuracy for edge and corner defects.  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_010_1] 中文：例如,Singh和Yadav[35]报告使用图像处理和瓷砖图像数学操作的精确度为96%,Matić等人[48] 在边缘和角缺陷方面准确度达到90%。

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_011_1] EN: However, these above traditional image processing techniques are effective for detecting defects on uniform surfaces, they struggle in real-world scenarios with varying defect shapes, sizes, and textures. Extracting a fixed set of features limits adaptability across diverse products. To overcome these challenges, ML algorithms have been employed for their ability to manage such variability within a scalable and robust framework.  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_011_1] 中文：然而,上述传统图像处理技术对于发现统一表面的缺陷是有效的,它们在现实世界中挣扎着,其形状、大小和质地各不相同。 抽取一套固定特征限制了不同产品之间的适应性。 为了克服这些挑战,采用了ML算法,以使其能够在一个可扩缩和稳健的框架内管理这种变异性。

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_012_1] EN: 4.2. Classical machine learning based techniques  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_012_1] 中文：4.2. 经典机器学习技术

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_013_1] EN: In this section, various classical ML algorithms that include classification, regression and clustering are discussed [94,95]. They can be categorised into three major groups based on their learning strategy: supervised learning, unsupervised learning, and semi-supervised learning. Another technique called the hybrid method is also used to increase the performance in learning. Table 7 summarises the accuracies for these ML algorithms across studies.  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_013_1] 中文：本节讨论了各种传统的ML算法,其中包括分类、回归和分组[94,95]。这些算法可以根据其学习战略分为三大类:监督学习、无监督的学习和半监督的学习。另一个称为混合方法的技术也用于提高学习绩效。表7总结了这些ML算法在跨研究中的适应性。

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_014_1] EN: 4.2.1. Supervised learning  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_014_1] 中文：4.2.1. 监督学习

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_015_1] EN: Supervised learning [69,111] is commonly used for defect classification using labelled data, where models are trained on known datasets and validated on unseen data. Support Vector Machine (SVM) has shown strong performance in applications, such as laser welding, copper strip inspection [66,101,102], and steel defect classification in aerospace and automotive domains. [111]. Mandriota et al. [99] uses SVM with gabor and wavelet features for rail defect detection, where gabor filters outperformed others. The K-Nearest Neighbour (KNN) algorithm achieves 96% accuracy in fabric defect detection using greylevel co-occurrence matrix features [96], and is also applied to gear crack detection with feature selection [97]. Random forest and logistic regression, combined with interpretability tools, is used by Oliveira et al. [104] for tire defect detection with 96% accuracy. Zorić et al. [112] applies fourier spectrum annuli for tile defects, achieving a 99% F1-score. A fused discrete cosine transform and discrete wavelet transform-based ANN is employed by Hemalatha et al. [105] for textile inspection, while Anupriya et al. [106] uses one-class SVM to achieve over 95% accuracy. In steel surface inspection, Dorbane et al. [108] applies synthetic minority over-sampling technique (SMOTE) with ensemble methods, XGBoost, for robust classification. Principal component analysis (PCA) with SVM is used by Li et al. [107] to classify reflective steel ball defects with 97.15% accuracy. Finally, the Naive Bayes (NB) classifier achieves over 98% accuracy in steel surface defect classification [5], highlighting its lightweight and efficient performance.  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_015_1] 中文：监督性学习[69,111]通常用于使用标签数据进行缺陷分类,模型在已知数据集方面得到训练,对看不见的数据进行验证。支持性矢量机(SVM)在激光焊接、铜条检查[66,101,102]和航空航天和汽车领域钢条缺陷分类等应用中表现出很强的性能。 [111] Mandriota等人(99)利用带有加博和波浪特征的SVM来探测铁路缺陷,而Gabor过滤器在其中排除了其他功能。 K-NN(K-NNN)算法利用灰度共同发生矩阵特征,在发现织物缺陷方面达到96%的准确性[96],并且还用于用特性选择检测齿轮裂缝[97];Olivira等人使用随机森林和后勤回归以及可解释工具[104],以96%的精确度探测轮胎缺陷。Zorić等人[112]对瓦砾缺陷应用四倍频谱,达到99%的F1芯片。 Hemalatha等人[105] 使用离散的余弦变形和离散的波纹变换ANN,进行纺织品检查,而Anupriya等人[106] 使用单级SVM达到95%的精度。在钢质表面检查中,Dorbane等人[108] 采用合成少数民族混合采样技术(SMOTE)XGBoost,以进行可靠的分类。 李等人([107] 使用SVM的主要部件分析(PCA)对反射钢球缺陷进行分类,精确度为97.15%;最后,Nive Bayes(NB)分类在钢表面缺陷分类中达到98%以上,突出其轻度和高效性能。

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_016_1] EN: 4.2.2. Unsupervised learning  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_016_1] 中文：4.2.2. 无监督的学习

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_017_1] EN: Unsupervised learning algorithms for defect detection work on unlabelled data and often rely on clustering methods to identify patterns. K- Means clustering, which groups data into K clusters based on similarity, has been effectively applied to defect detection tasks in semiconductor materials, fabrics, and machine fault diagnosis [98,100,113], achieving up to 95% accuracy in fabric defect detection [100]. Another widely used approach is the Self Organising Map (SOM), which maps high-dimensional inputs to a lower-dimensional space while preserving topological relationships. SOM has been employed for detecting wood surface defects using colour and texture features [114–116], with Silvén et al. [103] applying it to identify defects on lumber boards. Although SOM-based methods yield promising results, they typically require a sufficient number of defect samples to achieve high accuracy.  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_017_1] 中文：在未贴标签的数据中进行缺陷检测的无监督的学习算法工作,并常常依靠聚类方法来识别模式。 K- Means 集群(根据相似性将数据分组为K组)已被有效应用于半导体材料、布料和机器故障诊断中的缺陷检测任务[98 100 113],在织物缺陷检测中达到95%的准确度 [100] 。 另一个广泛采用的方法是自组织映射(SOM ) , 该图绘制了向低维空间的高维输入值,同时保持了地形关系。 SOM是用来利用颜色和质地特征[114-116] 探测木材表面缺陷的,Silvén等人[103] 用它来查明木材板上的缺陷,尽管基于SOM的方法产生有希望的结果,但通常需要足够数量的缺陷样本才能达到很高的精确度。

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_018_1] EN: 4.2.3. Hybrid methods  
[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_018_1] 中文：4.2.3. 混合法

[PDF 第 8 页 | B008_019_1] EN: To improve the performance of defect detection and classification, hybrid methods integrate two or more ML techniques. [PDF 第 8 页 | B008_019_1] 中文：为了改进缺陷检测和分类的性能,混合方法结合了两种或多种 ML 技术,这在许多研究中应用过:SOM 与 SVM 相结合用于探测瓦氏[117] 上的缺陷模式,遗传算法与 SVM 相结合用于探测

[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_147_1] EN: Fig. 7. Chronological overview of defect detection on different products using image processing and classical machine learning.  
[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_147_1] 中文：图7. 利用图像处理和古典机器学习对不同产品缺陷检测的时序概览。

[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_148_1] EN: aluminium [68] while the combination of KNN and CNN is used to identify unknown class defects on wafers [69]. Madhavan et al. [109] integrates features extracted from DenseNet201 with tree-based classifiers, reaching 99.72% on the NEU surface defect dataset.  
[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_148_1] 中文：Madhavan等人([109])将DenseNet201的特征与基于树木的分类器合并,在NEU表面缺陷数据集中达到99.72%。

[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_149_1] EN: A chronological overview of the most relevant method of defect detection on different products using image processing and ML is shown in Fig. 7. One of the advantages of ML algorithms is their ability to achieve good results even with limited sample data. However, a limitation of these algorithms is their reliance on explicitly engineered features, which can be challenging to acquire in complex cases. In real-world conditions, these methods perform excellent under some specific conditions (e.g., parameter setting) but are sensitive to variations. 
[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_149_1] 中文：7. 图7显示了利用图像处理和ML对不同产品进行最相关的缺陷检测方法的按时间顺序排列的概况。 ML算法的一个优点是,即使抽样数据有限,也能够取得良好效果;然而,这些算法的局限性在于依赖明确设计的特征,在复杂的情况下很难获得这些特征。

[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_150_1] EN:  In contrast, DL-based model overcomes these limitations by automatically extracting features and classifying defects, requiring only ground truth data. It also offers greater adaptability across various defect types and applications.  
[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_150_1] 中文：反之,基于DL的模型通过自动提取特征和对缺陷进行分类而克服了这些限制,只需要地面事实数据。 它也为各种缺陷类型和应用提供了更大的适应性。

[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_151_1] EN: 5. Efficacy of DL-based automated defect detection  
[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_151_1] 中文：5. 以DL为基础的自动缺陷检测的有效性

[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_152_1] EN: DL is rapidly advancing in the AI field for defect classification and localisation. Numerous studies [20,43,44,118–120] have applied DL across various product domains. This section critically reviews the use of DL in defect inspection covering both supervised and unsupervised methods for classification. 
[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_152_1] 中文：深度学习在缺陷分类和定位的人工智能领域正在迅速发展。许多研究 [20,43,44,118–120] 已将深度学习应用于各个产品领域。本节批判性地回顾了深度学习在缺陷检查中的使用，涵盖监督和非监督分类方法。

[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_153_1] EN: 9  
[PDF 第 9 页 | B009_153_1] 中文：9

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_006_1] 中文：监督学习

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_007_1] EN: Classification [49,50,70,119,121–123] Pre-trained  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_007_1] 中文：分类 [49,50,70,119,121–123] 预训练

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_008_1] EN: model (VGGNet, Alexnet etc.), CNN.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_008_1] 中文：模型（VGGNet、Alexnet 等）、CNN。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_009_1] EN: Require only image-based labelling; has a high learning capability; good classification accuracy; high speed training.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_009_1] 中文：仅需要基于图像的标签；具有较高的学习能力；良好的分类精度；高速训练。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_010_1] EN: Cannot detect defect location.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_010_1] 中文：无法检测缺陷位置。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_011_1] EN: Localisation [46,47,74,124–127]  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_011_1] 中文：定位 [46,47,74,124–127]

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_012_1] EN: Semantic Network (FCN and U-Net).  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_012_1] 中文：语义网络（FCN 和 U-Net）。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_013_1] EN: Can detect tiny defective areas.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_013_1] 中文：可以检测微小的缺陷区域。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_014_1] EN: Requires of pixel-level annotation and time-consuming.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_014_1] 中文：需要像素级标注且耗时。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_015_1] EN: Unsupervised Learning  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_015_1] 中文：无监督学习

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_016_1] EN: Classification [128–130]  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_016_1] 中文：分类[128–130]

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_017_1] EN: CNN with GAN. Can learn from unlabelled or partially labelled data; no need for annotation.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_017_1] 中文：CNN 与 GAN。可以从未标记或部分标记的数据中学习；无需标注。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_018_1] EN: Need to comprise in detection accuracy; cannot find the defective area.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_018_1] 中文：需要包括检测精度；找不到缺陷区域。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_019_1] EN: Localisation [24,131,132]  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_019_1] 中文：定位 [24,131,132]

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_020_1] EN: Hybridised model, CycleGAN.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_020_1] 中文：混合模型，CycleGAN。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_021_1] EN: No annotation is needed at the pixel-level; can be used in real-time application.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_021_1] 中文：像素级不需要标注；可以在实时应用中使用。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_022_1] EN: Training and validation are slow.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_022_1] 中文：训练和验证速度很慢。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_023_1] EN: Note: FCN: Fully Convolutional Network, GAN: Generative Adversarial Network.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_023_1] 中文：注：FCN：全卷积网络，GAN：生成对抗网络。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_024_1] EN: It also examines segmentation and detection  techniques for localisation, including encoder–decoder, pyramid, and attention-based networks for segmentation, and pyramid and attention-based YOLO models for detection. Table 8 summarises the strengths and limitations of these approaches. Beyond these, semi-supervised, weakly supervised, and self-supervised learning approaches are also gaining attraction for reducing annotation demands while maintaining high performance. Additionally, advanced architectures such as transformers, foundation models, few-shot learning, and LLMs are increasingly being integrated into defect inspection pipelines, enabling generalisation and interactive defect analysis.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_024_1] 中文：它还检查分割和检测定位技术，包括用于分割的编码器-解码器、金字塔和基于注意力的网络，以及用于检测的金字塔和基于注意力的 YOLO 模型。表 8 总结了这些方法的优点和局限性。除此之外，半监督、弱监督和自监督学习方法也因在保持高性能的同时减少标注需求而受到关注。此外，Transformer、基础模型、小样本学习和LLMs等先进架构越来越多地集成到缺陷检查流程中，从而实现泛化和交互式缺陷分析。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_025_1] EN: 5.1. Defect classification methods  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_025_1] 中文：5.1.缺陷分类方法

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_026_1] EN: The goal of defect classification network is to classify different types of defects on the surface of the product. The most popular model is supervised learning, which can achieve significant defect detection accuracy when given a large training dataset.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_026_1] 中文：缺陷分类网络的目标是对产品表面不同类型的缺陷进行分类。最流行的模型是监督学习，当给定大型训练数据集时，它可以实现显着的缺陷检测精度。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_027_1] EN: 5.1.1. Supervised learning (CNN-based methods)  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_027_1] 中文：5.1.1.监督学习（基于 CNN 的方法）

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_028_1] EN: Supervised CNN are considered as popular methods for both defect classification and localisation. They have been applied in various products such as on LED chips (95% accuracy [119]), laser welding in battery (99.87% accuracy [122]), fabric (95% accuracy [49], 98.1% accuracy [50]) and fabric print [38]. Park et al. [121] employs a supervised DCNN model to detect scratches on mobile phone screen covers and achieve an accuracy of 99% using a dataset of 16,800 labelled images. Other supervised DCNN model is employed by Dorafshan et al. [93] for crack detection on concrete and achieved 99% accuracy. Another work for crack detection on concrete has been done by Cha et al. [133] without calculating the defect features. Their method achieves good accuracy. Moreover, crack detection on concrete using DCNN model by Golding et al. [134] achieves 99% accuracy.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_028_1] 中文：受监督的CNN被认为是常见的缺陷分类和定位方法,在LED芯片(95%的准确度[119])、电池中的激光焊接(99.87%的准确度[122])、织物(95%的准确度[49]、98.1%的准确度[50])和布料印刷[38]等产品中应用了这些技术。 Park等人使用受监督的DCNN模型来检测移动电话屏幕覆盖上的抓痕并使用16 800个标签图像数据集达到99%的精确度。 多拉夫尚等人使用其他受监管的DCNN模型,对混凝土进行裂缝检测并达到99%的精确度;查等人在未计算缺陷特征的情况下进行了另外一项混凝土裂缝检测工作[133],其方法非常准确;此外,Golding等人使用DCNN模型对混凝土进行裂缝检测也达到了99%的精确度。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_029_1] EN: Ouyang et al. [39] proposes a system where they applies image pre-processing, fabric motif determination, candidate defect map generation and CNN to determine the defects in the fabric. Their method detect defects with 98% accuracy. Almeida et al. [49] introduces a cost-effective method for defect detection on fabric with high accuracy. They develop a new CNN model from scratch and incorporate a false negative reduction technique to enhance the performance of the system. Their system achieves an accuracy of 95%. Chakraborty et al. [38] proposes a DCNN method for identifying the spot (e.g., printing defect) and mismatch print on fabric. Tabernik et al. [135] proposes a two-stage surface inspection model combining a segmentation network with a DCNN for defect classification. Similarly, Ren et al. [136] uses a pre-trained CNN to extract patch-based features for defect classification. In order to deal with erroneous annotation data and image uncertainty, 
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_029_1] 中文：Ouyang等人[39] 提议建立一个系统,在其中他们应用图像预处理、织物图确定、候选缺陷地图生成和CNN来确定纤维中的缺陷,其方法以98%的精确度探测缺陷。Almeida等人[49] 采用了高精度检测织物缺陷的具有成本效益的方法。它们从零开始开发一个新的CNN模型,并采用虚假的负减少技术来提高系统的性能。它们的系统达到95%的准确率。 Chakraborty等人[38] 提议采用DCNN方法来识别斑点(如印刷缺陷)和织物上的不匹配打印。Tabernik等人[135] 提议采用两阶段表面检查模式,将分割网与DCNN相结合,进行缺陷分类。同样,Ren等人[136] 使用经过预先训练的CNN来提取瑕疵分类的图像块特性。为了处理错误的注解数据和图像不确定性,。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_030_1] EN: Yu  et al. [137] presents a DCNN model to handle label noise added by human annotators. In their work, noise coming from the surroundings is eliminated by using a symmetric residual filtering module. Then a combined probabilistic classification model is trained using the improved dataset to identify surface flaws in PCBs. He et al. [138] also proposes a DCNN method combined with a multilevel feature fusion network on steel surface to classify the defects. Their experimental result shows that it improves accuracy than other CNN.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_030_1] 中文：Yu等人[137]介绍了一个处理由人类标记员添加的标签噪音的DCNN模型。在他们的工作中,使用对称残余过滤模块消除了来自周围的噪音。然后用改进后的数据集训练了一个综合概率分类模型,以查明多氯联苯的表面缺陷。他等人[138]还提出了一种DCNN方法,该方法与钢质表面的多级特征聚合网络相结合,以对缺陷进行分类。其实验结果表明,它比其他CNN提高了准确性。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_031_1] EN: 5.1.2. Supervised learning (Transfer-learning-based models)  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_031_1] 中文：5.1.2. 监督学习（基于迁移学习的模型）

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_032_1] EN: In the pretrained model, it is difficult to configure a model by choosing the depth of the model and Kernel size. To configure a defect detection model, Konovalenko et al. [139] explores 14 neural network models for defect detection on the surface of the metal. They implement various models including InceptionV3, Inception-ResNetV2, ResNet, DenseNet, EfficientNet, MobileNet and U-Net to detect defects. Their study reveals that DenseNet and ResNet models perform very well with a depth of 50 layers, and exhibit the strongest generalisation capabilities for defect detection.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_032_1] 中文：在预先训练的模型中,很难通过选择模型深度和内核大小来配置模型。要配置缺陷检测模型,Konovalenko等人[139] 将14个神经网络模型用于金属表面的缺陷检测,他们采用各种模型,包括InvitionV3、Inption-ResNetV2、ResNet、DenseNet、高效网络、移动网络和U-Net,以检测缺陷。它们的研究表明,DenseNet和ResNet模型在50层深度上运行良好,并展示了最强的缺陷检测通用能力。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_033_1] EN: Singh and Desai [140] reduces computation for steel defect detection by extracting features with ResNet-101 and classifying them using SVM, improving results through data augmentation despite limited samples. For fabric inspection, Jun et al. [23] proposes a two-step learning-based method involving local and global defect prediction. They augment data, enhance images via histogram equalisation, use Inception-V1 for local prediction, and LeNet-5 for final classification. Liu et al. [50] modifies VGG16 to improve recognition of complex textile defects, achieving 98.1% accuracy and faster processing.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_033_1] 中文：Singh 和 Desai [140] 通过使用 ResNet-101 提取特征并使用 SVM 对它们进行分类，减少了钢材缺陷检测的计算量，尽管样本有限，但通过数据增强改进了结果。 For fabric inspection, Jun et al. [23]提出了一种基于两步学习的方法，涉及局部和全局缺陷预测。它们增强数据，通过直方图均衡增强图像，使用 Inception-V1 进行局部预测，并使用 LeNet-5 进行最终分类。刘等人。 [50]修改了VGG16以提高对复杂纺织品缺陷的识别，实现了98.1%的准确率和更快的处理速度。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_034_1] EN: In the industry perspective, sometime sophisticated feature extraction and modelling techniques are needed to distinguish between small variations and real faults. Mewada et al. [141] proposes a CNN approach based on residual networks to improve fabric defect classification. ResNet50, a pretrained residual network, is refined to categorise fabric defects into four groups. The method achieves 95.36% accuracy.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_034_1] 中文：从行业角度来看，有时需要复杂的特征提取和建模技术来区分微小变化和真正的故障。梅瓦达等人。 [141]提出了一种基于残差网络的 CNN 方法来改进织物缺陷分类。 ResNet50 是一种预训练残差网络，经过改进，可将织物缺陷分为四组。 The method achieves 95.36% accuracy.

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_035_1] EN: These both methods provide significant defect classification accuracy in diverse patterns of defects. However, it is a great challenge to collect a comprehensive representation of all possible defect patterns. This leads to imbalanced datasets where certain categories may have limited samples, making it challenging to adequately labelled all data. To address this limitation unsupervised model prove to be advantageous.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_035_1] 中文：这两种方法在不同的缺陷模式中都提供了显着的缺陷分类准确性。然而，收集所有可能的缺陷模式的全面表示是一个巨大的挑战。这会导致数据集不平衡，某些类别的样本可能有限，从而难以充分标记所有数据。为了解决这个限制，无监督模型被证明是有利的。

[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_036_1] EN: 5.1.3. Hybridised models  
[PDF 第 10 页 | B010_036_1] 中文：5.1.3.混合模型

[PDF 第 11 页 | B011_100_1] EN: a neural network to detect non-printing defects on fabric and achieves 98.6% accuracy. Chen et al. [42] proposes a hybrid model to classify defects (e.g., high, normal, overlong, and short) on wood surfaces. The suggested WDD-DL system incorporates various techniques for pre-processing in the first stage and various DCNN models in the second stage to detect defects. Damacharla et al. [52] employs a CNN model for automatic steel defect detection and they further enhance the performance by utilising a hybrid method called TLU-Net. Dharmawan et al. [145] uses an ensemble DL technique for defect shape classification with good accuracy (e.g., 99.03%). In another work, a novel defect detection network employs for surface inspection where features are extracted using labelled double-VGG16 [146]. It provides the highest detection accuracy but is time-consuming.  
[PDF 第 11 页 | B011_100_1] 中文：神经网络检测织物上的非印刷缺陷，准确率达到 98.6%。陈等人。 [42]提出了一种混合模型来对木材表面的缺陷（例如，高、正常、超长和短）进行分类。建议的 WDD-DL 系统在第一阶段结合了各种预处理技术，在第二阶段结合了各种 DCNN 模型来检测缺陷。达马查拉等人。 [52]采用 CNN 模型进行自动钢缺陷检测，并通过利用称为 TLU-Net 的混合方法进一步提高了性能。达玛万等人。 [145]使用集成深度学习技术进行缺陷形状分类，具有良好的准确性（例如，99.03%）。在另一项工作中，一种新颖的缺陷检测网络用于表面检查，其中使用标记的双 VGG16 提取特征[146]。它提供最高的检测精度，但非常耗时。

[PDF 第 11 页 | B011_101_1] EN: Altogether hybrid model provides good accuracy but it is a slow training process and requires a substantial amount of data. Moreover, the proposed model has some limitations, especially on tasks such as the localisation of defects in complex textures. Table 9 offers a comprehensive overview of the key aspects related to defect classification in various articles using supervised technique. The table provides information on the achieved accuracy of various methods, the dataset size utilised in the experiments, and the methodologies employed by each of the respective studies.  
[PDF 第 11 页 | B011_101_1] 中文：总体而言，混合模型提供了良好的准确性，但训练过程缓慢并且需要大量数据。此外，所提出的模型存在一些局限性，特别是在复杂纹理中的缺陷定位等任务上。表 9 全面概述了使用监督技术的各种文章中与缺陷分类相关的关键方面。该表提供了有关各种方法所达到的准确性、实验中使用的数据集大小以及每项研究所采用的方法的信息。

[PDF 第 11 页 | B011_102_1] EN: 5.1.4. Unsupervised learning  
[PDF 第 11 页 | B011_102_1] 中文：5.1.4.无监督学习

[PDF 第 11 页 | B011_103_1] EN: Most recognised models for unsupervised learning are autoencoderbased (AE) and GAN-based model. For example, Zenati et al. [147] trains a GAN-based model for defect detection more efficiently on high-dimensional datasets. Lian et al. [128] proposes a hybrid model combining CNN and GAN to detect small surface defects. GANs have also been used to generate synthetic data for surface inspection tasks [129, 130]. To enhance detection speed, the fast AnoGAN [148] is introduced, though such methods still struggle with complex patterns. A CycleGAN-based method [149] is used to annotate defective pixels and train a U-Net model, demonstrating promising results across various surface types. Yu [150] proposes an improved AE-based feature learning method for wafer defect detection, achieving 97% accuracy. Mujeeb et al. [151] applies AE for IC defect recognition with 85% sensitivity and 7% specificity, enabling training from a single sample via data augmentation. Haselmann et al. [152] reports 98% AUC for unsupervised surface inspection of plastic parts. Shakir and Topal [153] introduces an unsupervised spectral domain method for fabric fault detection using patch-based spectral comparison, reaching 94% accuracy and contributing a dataset of ten fabrics with 27 fault types. Tian and Li [154] uses AE-based pixel-wise classification by encoding/decoding  
[PDF 第 11 页 | B011_103_1] 中文：大多数公认的无监督学习模型是基于自编码器 (AE) 和基于 GAN 的模型。例如，泽纳蒂等人。 [147]训练基于 GAN 的模型，以便在高维数据集上更有效地进行缺陷检测。廉等人。 [128]提出了一种结合 CNN 和 GAN 的混合模型来检测小的表面缺陷。 GAN 还被用来生成表面检查任务的合成数据 [129, 130]。为了提高检测速度，引入了快速 AnoGAN [148]，尽管此类方法仍然难以应对复杂的模式。基于 CycleGAN 的方法 [149] 用于标注缺陷像素并训练 U-Net 模型，在各种表面类型上展示了有希望的结果。 Yu[150]提出了一种改进的基于AE的特征学习方法用于晶圆缺陷检测，达到了97%的准确率。穆吉布等人。 [151] applies AE for IC defect recognition with 85% sensitivity and 7% specificity, enabling training from a single sample via data augmentation.哈塞尔曼等人。 [152] 报道了塑料部件无监督表面检测的 98% AUC。 Shakir and Topal [153] introduces an unsupervised spectral domain method for fabric fault detection using patch-based spectral comparison, reaching 94% accuracy and contributing a dataset of ten fabrics with 27 fault types. Tian 和 Li [154] 通过编码/解码使用基于 AE 的像素级分类

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_043_1] EN: patches with latent variables, improving defect localisation by adjusting the latent space. Table 10 presents the summary of the aforementioned papers with their respective applications and functionalities.  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_043_1] 中文：使用潜在变量进行修补，通过调整潜在空间来改进缺陷定位。表 10 总结了上述论文及其各自的应用和功能。

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_044_1] EN: 5.1.5. Discussion  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_044_1] 中文：5.1.5。讨论

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_045_1] EN: A common characteristic observed in this category is the predominant use of supervised learning approaches, which necessitate labelled data for the training stage which is the main challenge in this category. Unsupervised learning significantly reduce the effort required for labelling and manual annotation of image datasets at the pixel level, as they do not rely on a large number of labelled defective sample. The primary drawback of unsupervised models is their lower accuracy and reliability compared to supervised learning.  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_045_1] 中文：A common characteristic observed in this category is the predominant use of supervised learning approaches, which necessitate labelled data for the training stage which is the main challenge in this category. Unsupervised learning significantly reduce the effort required for labelling and manual annotation of image datasets at the pixel level, as they do not rely on a large number of labelled defective sample.与监督学习相比，无监督模型的主要缺点是其准确性和可靠性较低。

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_046_1] EN: 5.2. Defect segmentation techniques  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_046_1] 中文：5.2.缺陷分割技术

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_047_1] EN: Another challenge in defect localisation is defect segmentation, which is utilised to assess, locate and quantify flaws. These elements are also necessary for a thorough evaluation of product quality [124, 125,155,156]. Therefore, in this section, several research articles are highlighted that have employed DL-based segmentation techniques for defect localisation in these aspects. Table 11 presents the key features of papers in the field of encoder–decoder, pyramid network and attention-based categories for defect segmentation.  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_047_1] 中文：缺陷定位的另一个挑战是缺陷分割，用于评估、定位和量化缺陷。这些要素对于全面评估产品质量也是必要的 [124, 125,155,156]。 Therefore, in this section, several research articles are highlighted that have employed DL-based segmentation techniques for defect localisation in these aspects. Table 11 presents the key features of papers in the field of encoder–decoder, pyramid network and attention-based categories for defect segmentation.

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_048_1] EN: 5.2.1. Encoder–decoder based supervised segmentation  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_048_1] 中文：5.2.1.基于编码器-解码器的监督分割

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_049_1] EN: Supervised segmentation models, a fully convolutional network (FCN), is initially proposed with effective learning [157]. By using this concept of FCN, Balzategui et al. [158] develop a FCN architecture to generate a defect segmentation map and identify the region of the defect accurately. Zambal et al. [126] uses semantic segmentation technique to implement an automated fibre placement defect segmentation. Liu et al. [125] also uses semantic segmentation for defect detection on fabric with 99.6% accuracy. Later, Ronneberger et al. [74] proposes another semantic segmentation model, U-Net, which is used by several researchers in their studies for diverse sets of applications and obtain efficient results. By modifying the U-Net model and combining with regional proposal networks (RPN), Han et al. [127] proposes a hybrid approach to identify defect regions on silicon wafers. In another research [124], a new end-to-end trainable model based on segmentation network is proposed for crack detection using multi-scale feature learning. Their proposed method outperforms the U-Net model with low cost and good accuracy. For surface defect detection, Lu et al. [159] suggests a real-time segmentation-based method where MobileNetV2 is used to extract features then reverse attention  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_049_1] 中文：监督分割模型，即完全卷积网络（FCN），最初是通过有效学习提出的[157]。 Balzategui 等人利用 FCN 的概念。 [158]开发了一种FCN架构来生成缺陷分割图并准确识别缺陷区域。赞巴尔等人。 [126]使用语义分割技术来实现自动纤维铺放缺陷分割。刘等人。 [125]还使用语义分割对织物进行缺陷检测，准确率达到 99.6%。后来，Ronneberger 等人。 [74]提出了另一种语义分割模型U-Net，该模型被一些研究人员在他们的研究中用于不同的应用集并获得了有效的结果。 Han等人通过修改U-Net模型并结合区域提议网络（RPN）。 [127]提出了一种混合方法来识别硅晶圆上的缺陷区域。在另一项研究[124]中，提出了一种基于分割网络的新的端到端可训练模型，用于使用多尺度特征学习进行裂纹检测。他们提出的方法优于 U-Net 模型，成本低且精度高。对于表面缺陷检测，Lu 等人。 [159]提出了一种基于实时分割的方法，其中使用 MobileNetV2 提取特征然后反转注意力

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_050_1] EN: modules are used to focus on the key region. Finally, a boundary refinement module is used to detect defects. Regarding generalisability the suggested technique performs well but needs annotated datasets.  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_050_1] 中文：模块用于关注关键区域。最后，使用边界细化模块来检测缺陷。关于通用性，建议的技术表现良好，但需要带标注的数据集。

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_051_1] EN: Drass et al. [46] uses semantic segmentation with AI-generated masks to detect glass edge cracks, achieving 99% accuracy. Jing et al. [156] combines MobileNetV2 with semantic segmentation for fabric defects, while Ning et al. [160] applies a U-Net variant with multi-scale sensing for ceramic defects, reaching 95.13% mAP but depending heavily on mask accuracy. Bai et al. [161] enhances steel defect segmentation with group normalisation and split-attention, achieving 89.11% IoU. Neven and Goedemé [54] proposes a multi-branch U-Net for defect classification, though it struggles with foreground-background confusion. To capture broader context, Wang et al. [162] introduce a hybrid transformer model. Luo et al. [163] present I2GF-Net for complex textures with semantic enhancement but high computational demand. Chen et al. [164] develops A-Net for lightweight real-time segmentation, with limited success on diverse defect types. Zhu et al. [165] uses ResNet34 with depth-wise separable convolutions to reduce redundancy in wood defect detection. Moreover, there are some contributions on the pixel level segmentation have been done in the last few years. He and Liu [166] proposes a pixel-level segmentation model on the DAGM dataset and achieves an MIoU of 0.845. Another segmentation model named TAS2-Net is implemented by Liu and He [155] with the same dataset, increased MIoU of 0.869. To find the defective region, Cao et al. [167] also employs a pixel-wise segmentation network using multilevel feature module in the RSDD dataset and achieves 0.85 in terms of mIoU.  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_051_1] 中文：德拉斯等人。 [46]使用语义分割和人工智能生成的掩模来检测玻璃边缘裂纹，达到 99% 的准确率。静等人。 [156]将 MobileNetV2 与织物缺陷语义分割相结合，而 Ning 等人。 [160]应用了对陶瓷缺陷进行多尺度传感的 U-Net 变体，达到 95.13% mAP，但在很大程度上取决于掩模精度。白等人。 [161]通过组归一化和分割注意力增强钢缺陷分割，达到 89.11% 的 IoU。 Neven 和 Goedemé [54] 提出了一种用于缺陷分类的多分支 U-Net，尽管它难以解决前景-背景混淆的问题。为了捕捉更广泛的背景，Wang 等人。 [162]引入混合Transformer模型。罗等人。 [163]提出了 I2GF-Net，用于具有语义增强但计算需求高的复杂纹理。陈等人。 [164]开发了用于轻量级实时分割的A-Net，但在不同的缺陷类型上取得的成功有限。朱等人。 [165]使用带有深度可分离卷积的 ResNet34 来减少木材缺陷检测中的冗余。此外，过去几年在像素级分割方面也做出了一些贡献。他和Liu [166]在DAGM数据集上提出了像素级分割模型，并实现了0.845的MIoU。 Liu 和 He [155] 使用相同的数据集实现了另一个名为 TAS2-Net 的分割模型，MIoU 增加了 0.869。为了找到有缺陷的区域，Cao 等人。 [167]还采用了RSDD数据集中使用多级特征模块的像素级分割网络，并在mIoU方面达到了0.85。

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_052_1] EN: Encoder–decoder model, FCN and U-Net, deliver strong segmentation but faces practical limitations in industry due to reliance on dense annotations, sensitivity to class imbalance, and difficulty handling small or low-contrast defects. Lightweight variants improve speed but often reduce accuracy. Enhancements, for example, pyramid networks and attention modules improve multi-scale learning and defect focus, but it still add computational complexity. In most of the research works surveyed, it has been shown that U-Net performs better than FCN for segmentation networks. Pixel-level segmentation has also achieved significant success in defect segmentation.  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_052_1] 中文：Encoder–decoder model, FCN and U-Net, deliver strong segmentation but faces practical limitations in industry due to reliance on dense annotations, sensitivity to class imbalance, and difficulty handling small or low-contrast defects.轻量级变体可以提高速度，但通常会降低准确性。 Enhancements, for example, pyramid networks and attention modules improve multi-scale learning and defect focus, but it still add computational complexity.在大多数调查的研究工作中，已经表明 U-Net 在分割网络方面的表现优于 FCN。像素级分割在缺陷分割方面也取得了显着的成功。

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_053_1] EN: 5.2.2. Pyramid network-based supervised segmentation  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_053_1] 中文：5.2.2. Pyramid network-based supervised segmentation

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_054_1] EN: These methods focus on improving results through the use of multi-scale features. They obtain rich contextual information from lower scales and high resolution from the largest scale. However, challenges related to real-time processing are the primary issues in this category.  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_054_1] 中文：这些方法侧重于通过使用多尺度特征来改进结果。他们从较低的尺度获得丰富的上下文信息，从最大的尺度获得高分辨率。然而，与实时处理相关的挑战是这一类别的主要问题。

[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_055_1] EN: Lin et al. [168] addresses the issue of identifying small, low-contrast flaws using ResNet-34 as backbone and combine with global dynamic  
[PDF 第 12 页 | B012_055_1] 中文：林等人。 [168]解决了使用 ResNet-34 作为主干并结合全局动态来识别小的、低对比度缺陷的问题

[PDF 第 13 页 | B013_119_1] EN: convolution and multi-scale fusion features to extract spatial feature. Then, these features are combined with reverse attention modules to improve the model performance in identifying small surface defects. In order to achieve accurate results with a small number of training examples, Meng et al. [169] presents a semantic segmentation network with spatial attention for surface defect identification. The network uses a double branch architecture made up of a detail-semantic guide module, coupled spatial pyramid pooling, and a modified residual shrinkage module. The goal of the suggested framework is to minimise the need for a sizable annotated dataset for surface defect detection. Liu  
[PDF 第 13 页 | B013_119_1] 中文：卷积和多尺度融合特征来提取空间特征。然后，将这些特征与反向注意模块相结合，以提高模型识别小表面缺陷的性能。为了用少量的训练样例获得准确的结果，Meng 等人。 [169]提出了一种具有空间注意力的语义分割网络，用于表面缺陷识别。该网络采用双分支架构，由细节语义引导模块、耦合空间金字塔池和改进的残差收缩模块组成。建议框架的目标是最大限度地减少表面缺陷检测对大量带标注数据集的需求。刘

[PDF 第 13 页 | B013_120_1] EN: et al. [170] also uses pyramid network to extract multi-scale feature maps then these feature are given as an input into a lightweight CNN model with a limited number of parameters for segmentation.  
[PDF 第 13 页 | B013_120_1] 中文：等人。 [170]还使用金字塔网络来提取多尺度特征图，然后将这些特征作为输入提供给具有有限数量参数的轻量级CNN模型以进行分割。

[PDF 第 13 页 | B013_121_1] EN: These architectures often involve complex designs and high computational costs, which hinder their suitability for real-time or resourceconstrained environments. Despite efforts to reduce annotation requirements and parameter sizes, such as using lightweight backbones or spatial attention, the integration of multiple modules can still increase model latency and training complexity. Balancing accuracy with efficiency remains a key challenge for broader industrial adoption.  
[PDF 第 13 页 | B013_121_1] 中文：这些架构通常涉及复杂的设计和高昂的计算成本，这阻碍了它们对实时或资源受限环境的适用性。尽管努力减少标注要求和参数大小，例如使用轻量级主干或空间注意力，但多个模块的集成仍然会增加模型延迟和训练复杂性。平衡准确性与效率仍然是更广泛的工业采用的关键挑战。
