## PDF 第 14 页

[查看原 PDF 第 14 页](<AI-enabled defect detection in industrial products_ A comprehensive survey, key insights and future research challenges.pdf#page=14>)

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_001_1] EN: 5.2.3. Attention-based supervised segmentation  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_001_1] 中文：5.2.3.基于注意力的监督分割

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_002_1] EN: Attention mechanism allows the model to focus on specific sections of the input while executing a task. It dynamically assigns weights to different elements in the input, indicating their relative importance or relevance.  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_002_1] 中文：注意力机制允许模型在执行任务时关注输入的特定部分。它动态地为输入中的不同元素分配权重，指示它们的相对重要性或相关性。

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_003_1] EN: For example, Wang et al. [79] enhances tiny defect detection in semiconductors using CSPdarkNet53-SA with spatial attention, spatial pyramid pooling, and a path aggregation network to capture multi-scale fine-grained features. Similarly, Liu et al. [76] proposes an adaptive segmentation network that combines multi-scale defect features with dilated convolution and dual attention context guidance, effectively identifying small and texture-similar defects in real industrial datasets.  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_003_1] 中文：例如，王等人。 [79] 使用具有空间注意力、空间金字塔池和路径聚合网络的 CSPdarkNet53-SA 来增强半导体中的微小缺陷检测，以捕获多尺度细粒度特征。同样，刘等人。 [76]提出了一种自适应分割网络，将多尺度缺陷特征与扩张卷积和双重注意力上下文引导相结合，有效识别实际工业数据集中的小且纹理相似的缺陷。

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_004_1] EN: Recently, researchers have been increasingly focusing on improving pixel-level segmentation by incorporating attention mechanisms. For example, Zhong et al. [171] designs a deep Gaussian attention network for segmenting timber defects using Deeplabv3+. This network improves segmentation accuracy even in difficult locations by utilising a transformer-based attention mechanism to capture global information. The network handles ambiguous defects and different forms and sizes of defects by utilising astrous spatial pyramid pooling and incorporating a Gaussian module into attention mechanisms. Luo et al. [78] also develops a model where a feature pyramid network is used to extract features. Convolutional operators and memory attention-enhanced feature modules are used to generate a saliency map. Then, the features from saliency map are used to conduct prediction. The framework works excellently using a small number of samples with pixel-level annotation. Another pixel-level segmentation network is deployed by Shao et al. [132] on fabric and achieved 0.825 of MIoU. Due to a minimal portion image pixels of defects, the pixel-level defect detection task is always against class-imbalance issue which also will affect the detection performance. To address this issue Yang et al. [172] proposes an attention-based segmentation network to detect welding defects with a dice coefficient of 0.854.  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_004_1] 中文：最近，研究人员越来越关注通过结合注意力机制来改进像素级分割。例如，钟等人。 [171]使用 Deeplabv3+ 设计了一个深度高斯注意力网络来分割木材缺陷。该网络通过利用基于Transformer的注意力机制来捕获全局信息，即使在困难的位置也可以提高分割精度。该网络通过利用巨大的空间金字塔池并将高斯模块纳入注意机制来处理不明确的缺陷以及不同形式和大小的缺陷。罗等人。 [78]还开发了一个模型，其中使用特征金字塔网络来提取特征。卷积算子和记忆注意力增强特征模块用于生成显着性图。然后，使用显着图的特征进行预测。该框架使用少量带有像素级标注的样本运行良好。 Shao 等人部署了另一个像素级分割网络。 [132]在织物上实现了 0.825 的 MIoU。由于缺陷图像像素的比例极小，像素级缺陷检测任务始终面临类不平衡问题，这也会影响检测性能。为了解决这个问题，杨等人。 [172]提出了一种基于注意力的分割网络来检测骰子系数为0.854的焊接缺陷。

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_005_1] EN: These aforementioned methods perform excellently in defect localisation but still face problems in detection accuracy and data imbalance. Attention-based segmentation enhances defect detection by focusing on relevant regions, improving localisation of small or ambiguous defects in complex textures. Spatial, channel, and transformer-based attention modules boost contextual understanding and have been effectively integrated into models like U-Net and DeepLabv3+. However, their benefits come at the cost of increased complexity, memory use, and sensitivity to domain shifts and class imbalance, limiting real-time deployment unless carefully optimised.  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_005_1] 中文：上述这些方法在缺陷定位方面表现出色，但仍然面临检测精度和数据不平衡的问题。基于注意力的分割通过关注相关区域来增强缺陷检测，改善复杂纹理中小或模糊缺陷的定位。基于空间、通道和 Transformer 的注意力模块增强了上下文理解，并已有效集成到 U-Net 和 DeepLabv3+ 等模型中。然而，它们的好处是以增加复杂性、内存使用以及对域转移和类不平衡的敏感性为代价的，除非仔细优化，否则会限制实时部署。

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_006_1] EN: 5.2.4. Defect segmentation by unsupervised learning (GAN-based)  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_006_1] 中文：5.2.4.通过无监督学习（基于 GAN）进行缺陷分割

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_007_1] EN: This section highlights some studies on unsupervised or weekly supervised model. GAN-based models, such as cycleGAN and pixelGAN, are very widely used unsupervised model for defect segmentation. For example, Niu et al. [173] proposes a weakly supervised defect identification technique that locates and segments defect areas using image level annotations. To do this, they combine a defect attention module with CycleGAN. The generator is trained with test images for flaw detection through comparison using the suggested method. The experimental findings show excellent defect segmentation performance across a range of industrial applications. These approaches offer valuable alternatives for detecting surface defects without relying heavily on labelled data. Kim et al. [24] employs a pixelGAN-based model on semiconductor manufacturing data to identify the defect and segment the defective area. The comparison result shows that their method performs better than another base model in a real industry dataset.  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_007_1] 中文：本节重点介绍一些关于无监督或弱监督（原文写作 Weekly-supervised）模型的研究。基于GAN的模型，例如cycleGAN和pixelGAN，是非常广泛使用的用于缺陷分割的无监督模型。例如，牛等人。 [173]提出了一种弱监督缺陷识别技术，该技术使用图像级标注来定位和分割缺陷区域。为此，他们将缺陷注意模块与 CycleGAN 结合起来。生成器使用测试图像进​​行训练，通过使用建议的方法进行比较来进行缺陷检测。实验结果表明在一系列工业应用中具有出色的缺陷分割性能。这些方法为检测表面缺陷提供了有价值的替代方案，而无需严重依赖标记数据。金等人。 [24]在半导体制造数据上采用基于pixelGAN的模型来识别缺陷并分割缺陷区域。比较结果表明，他们的方法在实际行业数据集中比其他基本模型表现更好。

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_008_1] EN: Xu et al. [174] addresses limited training data in fabric defect detection by using GAN-based augmentation. By combining defect localisation maps, a dual U-Net, and multi-head attention, the model significantly improves accuracy across five defect types—float, line, knot, hole, and stain from 41%, 44%, 38%, 42%, and 41% to 78%, 76%, 72%, 67%, and 64% respectively.Wan et al. [175] proposes an  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_008_1] 中文：Xu等人(174)通过使用基于 GAN 的增强来应对织物缺陷检测方面的有限训练数据。 通过将缺陷定位图、双重 U-Net 和多头目关注结合起来,该模型大大提高了五种缺陷类型的准确性:浮标、线条、结节、洞和污点,分别从41%、44%、38%、42%和41%提高到78%、76%、72%、67%和64%。 Wan等人([175])建议采用

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_009_1] EN: unsupervised high-frequency feature mapping model that captures rich high-frequency details and uses a query-key attention module for defect comparison. Their approach improves average AUC by 5.3% in detection and 2.9% in segmentation, outperforming prior state-of-the-art fabric methods.  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_009_1] 中文：高频地貌绘图模型可以捕捉丰富的高频细节,并使用查询键关注模块进行缺陷比较。 其方法在检测中将AUC平均提高5.3%,在分解中提高2.9%,这比以往最先进的布料方法要好。

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_010_1] EN: 5.2.5. Discussion  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_010_1] 中文：5.2.5. 讨论

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_011_1] EN: All studies in this category focuses on segmenting defects in images. The encoder–decoder based networks has the highest percentage of deep learning in the literature. This indicates that a significant portion of recent research has focused on defect segmentation using encoder–decoder-based architectures, which improves the results in defect segmentation. An interesting aspect of this category is the use of unlabelled data, where some works explore unsupervised learning techniques. These methods demonstrate the ability to learn from unlabelled data, making defect detection more accessible and flexible. By incorporating unlabelled data, the encoder–decoder models become more robust and adaptable to different scenarios. However, there is room to further improve accuracy and applicability for real-time applications.  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_011_1] 中文：这一类中的所有研究都集中在图像的分解缺陷上。 以编码器-代码器为基础的网络在文献中的深层学习比例最高。 这说明最近相当一部分的研究侧重于使用编码器-代码器为基础的结构进行缺陷分类,从而改进了缺陷分类的结果。 这个类别的一个有趣的方面是使用未贴标签的数据,有些工作探索了无监督的学习技术。 这些方法显示了从无标签数据中学习的能力,使得缺陷检测更容易被利用和灵活化。 通过整合无标签数据,编码器-编码器模型变得更加强大和适应不同的情景。 但是,还可以进一步提高实时应用的准确性和适用性。

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_012_1] EN: 5.3. Defect detection techniques  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_012_1] 中文：5.3. 缺陷检测技术

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_013_1] EN: Defect detection in industrial vision is typically categorised into one-stage (e.g., SSD [81], YOLO [80]) and two-stage methods (e.g., Faster R-CNN [82]). For one-stage detection, Shafi et al. [176] uses SSD with MobileNet as the backbone for real-time steel defect detection. Zhang et al. [177] applies YOLO for detecting steel strip defects, leveraging anchor boxes and confidence maps for localisation. In two-stage approaches, Wang et al. [178] uses faster R-CNN for detecting small steel surface defects with 81% classification and 72% detection accuracy. Cha et al. [179] uses the same model for detecting concrete cracks and steel corrosion. Akhyar et al. [180] introduces a cascade R- CNN with a deformable region proposal network and random scaling to boost detection accuracy on steel surfaces. Recently, YOLO-based models have gained popularity due to their speed, with many studies enhancing performance using feature pyramids or integrating attention modules. This section highlights such developments.  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_013_1] 中文：工业视觉中的缺陷检测通常分为一个阶段(如SSD[81]、YOLO[80])和两个阶段方法(如快速R-CNN[82]);对于一个阶段的检测,Shafi等人[176]使用移动网络作为实时钢缺陷检测的主干。 在两阶段办法中,Wang等人([178] 使用更快的R-CNN来探测小钢表面缺陷,分类率为81%,检测精确度为72%。Cha等人(179)使用同样的模型来探测混凝土裂缝和钢腐蚀。Akhyar等人(180)采用级联R-CNN,配有可变区域建议网络和随机缩放,以提高钢表面的检测精度。最近,以YOLO为基础的模型因其速度而越来越受欢迎,许多研究利用地貌金字塔或综合关注模块提高性能。 本节着重介绍这些事态发展。

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_014_1] EN: 5.3.1. YOLO with pyramid-based networks  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_014_1] 中文：5.3.1. 拥有金字塔网络的YOLO

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_015_1] EN: This section focuses on defect detection using feature pyramid network with YOLO model. Liu et al. [40] proposes a YOLOv4 network for mapping small defective areas on the fabric where the spot is enclosed in the background. They use CLAHE to process the input image and spatial pyramid pooling structure instead of max pooling. It provides a real-time performance in industrial trials. However, limited resolution decrease the detection accuracy. Table 12 summarises papers that utilised the pyramid feature strategy for defect detection.  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_015_1] 中文：本节的重点是利用带有YOLO模型的地物金字塔网络进行缺陷检测。刘等人建议使用一个YOLOv4网络来映射背景中含有该点的小缺陷区域,他们利用CLAHE处理输入图像和空间金字塔集合结构,而不是最大集中。它提供了工业试验中的实时性能。然而,有限的分辨率降低了探测准确性。表12总结了使用金字塔特征战略查找缺陷的文件。

[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_016_1] EN: Han et al. [181] enhances YOLOv5 by integrating transformer modules with CSP-Darknet53, improving mAP by 3.1% for wood defects but struggling with fine cracks. An et al. [182] also works on wood defects by applying YOLOv8 with BiFormer, gaining 3.5% mAP at high computational cost. On other products, (e.g., LED, ceramic) Zhong et al. [183] proposes CM-YOLOv5 with multi-head self-attention, achieving a 3.8% mAP increase, while Chen et al. [184] uses BiFPN to reach 90.8% mAP. Chen et al. [185] uses improve YOLOv5 for ceramic defects, achieving 94.3% mAP through better multi-scale feature fusion. YOLOv8 later outperforms YOLOv5 in irregular surface environments. Zhao et al. [186] presents modified YOLOv8n for steel using dynamic coordinate attention and multi-scale blocks. Mei et al. [187] uses YOLOv8 for fabric defects using semantic fusion and enhanced attention, achieving 97.4% accuracy. Song et al. [188] further improves YOLOv8 with deformable convolution and C2F modules, boosting mAP by 6.9%. Despite these advances, most models still face challenges with detecting small, low-contrast defects in complex environments.  
[PDF 第 14 页 | B014_016_1] 中文：Han等人[181]通过将Transformer模块与CSP-Darknet53合并,使木材缺陷的 mAP改善3.1%,但遇到了细裂缝。An等人[182] 也利用YOLOv8和BiFormer来处理木材缺陷,以高计算成本获得3.5% mAP。关于其他产品,(如LED、陶瓷)Zhong等人[183] 提议CM-YOLOv5采用多头自省法,达到3.8% mAP,而Chen等人[184]则使用BiFPN达到90.8% mAP。 Chen等人[185] 使用改进的YOLOv5处理陶瓷缺陷,通过更好的多级特征聚合实现94.3%的 mAP。 YOLOv8后来在不规则的表面环境中优于YOLOV5。Zhao等人[186] 提出使用动态协调注意力和多级块来修改的YOLOV8n处理钢材。Mei等人[187] 利用语义结合和强化注意用YOLOv8处理织物缺陷,达到97.4%的精度。 Song等人[188年]进一步改进了YOLOv8,采用变形变形和C2F模块,将MAP提高6.9%。 尽管取得了这些进步,但大多数模型在发现复杂环境中的小、低相容缺陷方面仍然面临挑战。

[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_061_1] EN: defects is essential for ensuring product quality and preventing economic losses in high-tech industries. Du et al. [189] proposes adaptive focusing features extraction method to better capture morphological and spatial changes in defects, combined with a feature pyramid network to enhance adaptability. Their method achieves 83.5% mAP on the NEU-DET dataset, outperforming the baseline by 8.2%. Similarly, Peng et al. [190] introduces a double attention feature pyramid network with deformable convolutions in the early ResNet50 stages, achieving 87.9% average precision for steel defect detection. Despite these advances, further improvements are needed to handle defects with random shapes. For surface defect detection on multi-object scenarios, Zhang et al. [191] proposes a lightweight CNN model with a coordinate attention backbone and a bidirectional weighted feature pyramid for feature extraction. While effective in detection, it struggles with complex defects. Zhao et al. [192] introduces an attention-based multi-scale feature fusion module that enhances defect-background discrimination using cross and self-enhanced attention but lacks precision across diverse defect types and demands high computational resources. To improve defect localisation, Wan et al. [193] develops an enhanced YOLOv5 model incorporating spatial pyramid pooling and attention modules. Evaluated on a colour printing fabric dataset, it achieves 87.6% mAP, outperforming baseline models.  
[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_061_1] 中文：缺陷对于确保产品质量和防止高科技产业的经济损失至关重要。 Du等人[189]提议采用适应性突出特征提取方法,以更好地捕捉缺陷的形态和空间变化,同时结合一个地貌金字塔网络来增强适应性。其方法在NEU-DET数据集上实现了83.5%的 mAP,比基准值高出8.2%。同样,彭等人[190]引入了双重关注特征金字塔网络,在ResNet50早期阶段出现变形变异,实现87.9%的平均钢质缺陷检测精确度。 尽管取得了这些进步,但还需要进一步改进来处理随机形状的缺陷。对于多物体情景的表面缺陷检测,张等人[191] 提议采用轻量级CNN模型,该模型具有协调关注主干线和双向加权特征金字塔,用于地貌提取。它虽然在检测中有效,但与复杂的缺陷抗争。赵等人[192]引入了基于关注的多尺度特征聚合模块,该模块利用交叉和自我强化的关注来强化倒戈背景歧视,但在不同类型缺陷之间缺乏精确度,需要高额计算资源。 为改善缺陷定位,Wan等人[193]开发了一种包含空间金字塔共享和关注模块的增强型YOLOv5模型。

[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_062_1] EN: The studies in this category focuses on improving results through the use of multi-scale features. YOLO-based models have improved in accuracy with pyramid features and attention, reaching up to 97.4% mAP. While YOLOv5 enhances speed over YOLOv4, both struggled with small or low-contrast defects. YOLOv8 offers better accuracy, but challenges persist in real-time deployment, generalisation, and robustness in complex environments. Despite ongoing advancements, balancing accuracy, speed, and reliability remains key for industrial use. However, challenges related to generalisation and real-time processing are the primary issues in this category.  
[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_062_1] 中文：此类研究的重点是通过使用多尺度特征来改进结果。基于 YOLO 的模型通过金字塔特征和注意力提高了准确性，达到 97.4% mAP。虽然 YOLOv5 比 YOLOv4 提高了速度，但两者都面临着小或低对比度缺陷的问题。 YOLOv8 提供了更好的准确性，但在复杂环境中的实时部署、泛化和鲁棒性方面仍然存在挑战。尽管不断进步，但平衡精度、速度和可靠性仍然是工业应用的关键。然而，与泛化和实时处理相关的挑战是这一类别的主要问题。

[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_063_1] EN: 5.3.2. YOLO with attention-based models  
[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_063_1] 中文：5.3.2. YOLO 具有基于注意力的模型

[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_064_1] EN: To enhance defect detection, researchers have integrated attention mechanisms with YOLO models, particularly through multi-scale feature fusion to improve small defect recognition. Studies by Pan et al.  
[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_064_1] 中文：为了增强缺陷检测，研究人员将注意力机制与 YOLO 模型相结合，特别是通过多尺度特征融合来提高小缺陷识别。潘等人的研究。

[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_065_1] EN: [196], Li and Zhu [197], Mao et al. [198], Chen et al. [199], Cao et al. [200], and Li et al. [201] report notable gains in mAP, precision, recall, and F1-score across different surfaces such as ceramics, smartphone glass, magnetic tiles, and other glass products. Despite these advancements, challenges remain in detecting defects with extreme aspect ratios and in reducing reliance on labelled data. Additionally, Lu et al. [202] employs a sliding window approach, which struggles with large defects exceeding the window size. Table 14 summarises these attention-based detection efforts.  
[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_065_1] 中文：[196]，李和朱[197]，毛等人。 [198]，陈等人。 [199]，曹等人。 [200]，以及李等人。 [201]报告了陶瓷、智能手机玻璃、磁砖和其他玻璃产品等不同表面的 mAP、精度、召回率和 F1 分数显着提高。尽管取得了这些进步，但在检测具有极端纵横比的缺陷和减少对标记数据的依赖方面仍然存在挑战。此外，Lu 等人。 [202]采用滑动窗口方法，该方法难以处理超过窗口大小的大缺陷。表 14 总结了这些基于注意力的检测工作。

[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_066_1] EN: Cui et al. [203] proposes an improved YOLOv7-based network for detecting small wood surface defects, introducing a cascade efficient layer attention module that improves mAP by 2.1% over the base model. Similarly, Zhang et al. [204] presents a lightweight model using a novel neck layer and squeeze-and-excitation attention to boost wood defect detection accuracy to 76.5%, improving mAP by 2.9% over YOLOv8, though it struggles with certain defects like knots with cracks. Lei et al. [205] introduces DGNet, a flexible model for battery defect detection, combining lightweight feature fusion and ShuffleNet backbone to achieve 91.8% mAP with reduced computation. Zhou and Wang [206] enhances YOLO with a triplet convolutional coordinate attention module to capture cross-channel features, outperforming baselines on NEU-DET and GC10-DET. Yuan et al. [207] proposes a model for multi-category steel defects using faster block, ghost convolutions, and channel attention in C2f, showing strong results on NEU-DET, though it struggles with detecting crazing defects due to labelling issues.  
[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_066_1] 中文：崔等人。 [203]提出了一种基于YOLOv7的改进网络，用于检测木材表面的小缺陷，引入了级联高效层注意模块，该模块使mAP比基础模型提高了2.1%。同样，张等人。 [204]提出了一种轻量级模型，使用新颖的颈部层和挤压和激励注意力，将木材缺陷检测精度提高到 76.5%，比 YOLOv8 将 mAP 提高了 2.9%，尽管它在处理某些缺陷（如带裂纹的节子）时遇到了困难。雷等人。 [205]引入了DGNet，一种用于电池缺陷检测的灵活模型，结合轻量级特征融合和ShuffleNet主干，以减少计算量实现91.8％的mAP。 Zhou 和 Wang [206] 使用三元组卷积坐标注意模块增强了 YOLO，以捕获跨通道特征，优于 NEU-DET 和 GC10-DET 上的基线。袁等人。 [207]提出了一种在 C2f 中使用更快的块、幻影卷积和通道注意力的多类别钢缺陷模型，在 NEU-DET 上显示出很强的结果，尽管由于标签问题，它在检测银纹缺陷方面遇到了困难。

[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_067_1] EN: Ding and Yang [194] enhances YOLOv5 with constructive block attention and data augmentation to detect tiny defects on flat glass surfaces, achieving 98.36% and mAP 2.62% higher than the base model but does not address edge defects. Li and Yang [195] proposes a YOLOv5-based model for metal surface inspection using a hybrid attention module (e.g., channel, spatial) and dense connections, maintaining real-time performance with improved accuracy. Xu et al. [208] introduces a raster rotation imaging method for metal surfaces and integrates it into a YOLOv7-tiny model with multi-scale cross attention, improving mAP by 0.8% over the base model, though it still struggles  
[PDF 第 15 页 | B015_067_1] 中文：Ding 和 Yang [194] 通过构造性块注意力和数据增强增强了 YOLOv5，以检测平板玻璃表面上的微小缺陷，实现了比基本模型高 98.36% 的结果，mAP 提高了 2.62%，但没有解决边缘缺陷。 Li 和 Yang [195] 提出了一种基于 YOLOv5 的金属表面检测模型，使用混合注意力模块（例如通道、空间）和密集连接，保持实时性能并提高准确性。徐等人。 [208]引入了金属表面的光栅旋转成像方法，并将其集成到具有多尺度交叉注意力的YOLOv7-tiny模型中，与基础模型相比，mAP提高了0.8%，尽管仍然很困难

[PDF 第 16 页 | B016_098_1] EN: Table 13 summarises the advantages and limitations of various  
[PDF 第 16 页 | B016_098_1] 中文：表13总结了各种教育的优点和局限性。

[PDF 第 16 页 | B016_099_1] EN: YOLO variants, including those integrated with pyramid networks, attention modules for defect detection. From the table, it is revealed that, integrating attention mechanisms with YOLO models has led to improved performance in defect detection, particularly for small and subtle defects across surfaces, such as ceramics, glass, and metals. Enhancements such as channel and spatial attention, coordinate attention, and multi-scale fusion have significantly boosted metrics such as mAP and precision. Lightweight versions (e.g., DGNet, YOLOv7-tiny) also offer real-time potential with reduced computational load. However, these models still face limitations in detecting defects with extreme aspect ratios, irregular shapes, or poor annotations. Sliding window methods struggle with larger defects, and some attention-augmented models underperform on specific defect types (e.g., knot with crack or  
[PDF 第 16 页 | B016_099_1] 中文：YOLO变体,包括与金字塔网络结合的变异体、注意缺陷检测模块。从表中可以看出,将注意机制与YOLO模型结合后,发现缺陷的性能有所提高,特别是陶瓷、玻璃和金属等表面的微小和微妙缺陷。通道和空间注意力、协调关注和多尺度聚变等增强显著提升了 mAP 和精度等测量标准。轻量级版本(例如DGNet、YOLOv7-tiny)也提供了减少计算负荷的实时潜力。 然而,这些模型在发现极端侧面比率、不规则形状或说明差的缺陷方面仍然面临限制。 滑动窗口的方法与较大的缺陷抗争,一些引人注意的模型在特定缺陷类型(例如与裂缝或裂缝交错)上表现不佳。

[PDF 第 16 页 | B016_100_1] EN: crazing). Despite high accuracy in controlled settings, generalisability and robustness across diverse real-world scenarios remain ongoing challenges.  
[PDF 第 16 页 | B016_100_1] 中文：尽管在受控环境中具有很高的准确性,但在各种现实世界情景中,普遍性和稳健性仍然是持续的挑战。

[PDF 第 16 页 | B016_101_1] EN: 5.4. Unsupervised, semi-supervised and other learning for defect detection  
[PDF 第 16 页 | B016_101_1] 中文：5.4. 无监督、半监督和其他学习发现缺陷

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_022_1] EN: is further evaluated using YOLOv5. Ou et al. [209] addresses the challenge of data labelling and collection by proposing a StyleGAN3- based data augmentation method to generate ceramic defect samples, reducing training time and improving detection accuracy. Xie et al. [210] introduces HMTGAN, a CycleGAN–StyleGAN hybrid model, using latent space sampling and a novel loss function to generate high-quality defect samples, achieving strong results on crack datasets. Yang et al. [211] presents an unsupervised approach using a frequency shift convolutional auto-encoder trained on defect-free samples, addressing low sample size and reducing manual labelling effort. Table 15 presents a summary of selected papers in the field of GAN networks for defect detection. The studies conduct in this category have demonstrated promising results, particularly in the context of unsupervised and weakly supervised approaches. These approaches offer valuable alternatives for detecting defects without relying heavily on labelled data.  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_022_1] 中文：使用 YOLOv5 进一步评估。欧等人。 [209]通过提出一种基于StyleGAN3的数据增强方法来生成陶瓷缺陷样本，减少训练时间并提高检测精度，解决了数据标记和收集的挑战。谢等人。 [210]引入了HMTGAN，一种CycleGAN-StyleGAN混合模型，使用潜在空间采样和新颖的损失函数来生成高质量的缺陷样本，在裂纹数据集上取得了很好的结果。杨等人。 [211]提出了一种无监督方法，使用在无缺陷样本上训练的频移卷积自编码器，解决样本量较小的问题并减少手动标记工作。表 15 总结了用于缺陷检测的 GAN 网络领域的精选论文。此类研究已显示出有希望的结果，特别是在无监督和弱监督方法的背景下。这些方法为检测缺陷提供了有价值的替代方案，而无需严重依赖标记数据。

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_023_1] EN: 5.4.2. Diffusion-based models  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_023_1] 中文：5.4.2.基于扩散的模型

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_024_1] EN: Diffusion models are emerging as a powerful tools in industrial defect detection and augmentation, addressing data scarcity and improving robustness. These generative models learn to synthesise realistic images by denoising random noise through a learned reverse process, typically trained in an unsupervised fashion. MED-AGNeT [212] combines a conditional diffusion module with attention-based detection to improve accuracy on textile defect detection. Tang et al. [213] enhances unsupervised fabric defect localisation using timestep-adaptive denoising. In steel defect detection, Tai et al. [214] applies stable diffusion with token and domain adaptation to improve accuracy. Table 16 presents a summary of selected papers in the field of diffusion networks for defect detection. It is showed in the table that Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) and DiffAD are used to reconstruct normal patterns and isolate anomalies with pixel-level precision, as seen in both steel [215] and general industrial surface defect detection [216]. Hong et al. [217] uses YOLO-DSC with DDIM-based augmentation for efficient real-time detection. The overall study shows that diffusion models, whether used for data generation or reconstruction can substantially enhance unsupervised and semi-supervised defect detection pipelines.  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_024_1] 中文：扩散模型正在成为工业缺陷检测和增强的强大工具，可解决数据稀缺问题并提高稳健性。这些生成模型通过学习的反向过程（通常以无监督的方式进行训练）来学习通过对随机噪声进行去噪来合成逼真的图像。 MED-AGNeT [212] 将条件扩散模块与基于注意力的检测相结合，以提高纺织品缺陷检测的准确性。唐等人。 [213]使用时间步自适应去噪增强无监督织物缺陷定位。在钢材缺陷检测中，Tai 等人。 [214]应用带有令牌和域适应的稳定扩散来提高准确性。表 16 总结了缺陷检测扩散网络领域的选定论文。表中显示，潜在扩散模型（LDM）和 DiffAD 用于重建正常图案并以像素级精度隔离异常，如钢铁 [215] 和一般工业表面缺陷检测 [216] 中所见。洪等人。 [217]使用YOLO-DSC和基于DDIM的增强来实现高效的实时检测。总体研究表明，扩散模型，无论是用于数据生成还是重建，都可以大大增强无监督和半监督的缺陷检测流程。

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_025_1] EN: 5.4.3. Semi-supervised learning  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_025_1] 中文：5.4.3.半监督学习

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_026_1] EN: Semi-supervised learning has proven effective for defect detection in data-scarce industrial settings. Table 17 presents a summary of selected papers in this field. For example, Lei et al. [218] introduces a hybrid U-Net with CSPNet and CBAM, augmented with synthetic data through Poisson noise, achieving 98.22% mAP on GC10-DET. This approach demonstrates strong generalisation but may struggle in uncontrolled real-world conditions. Similarly, Song et al. [219] introduces MSD Net, combining CycleGAN-based domain transfer with pseudolabelling and multi-head self-attention, showing a 4.8% improvement over supervised baselines. However, its reliance on domain migration and attention modules introduces complexity. Another method, DifferNet [220] learns from only normal samples but suffers from noisy localisation on large defects. Cao et al. [221] presents a knowledge distillation method with abnormal-aware feature enhancement, though  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_026_1] 中文：事实证明，半监督学习对于数据稀缺的工业环境中的缺陷检测是有效的。表 17 总结了该领域的选定论文。例如，雷等人。 [218]引入了具有CSPNet和CBAM的混合U-Net，通过泊松噪声通过合成数据进行增强，在GC10-DET上实现了98.22%的mAP。这种方法表现出很强的通用性，但在不受控制的现实条件下可能会遇到困难。同样，宋等人。 [219] 引入了 MSD Net，将基于 CycleGAN 的域转移与伪标签和多头自注意力相结合，与监督基线相比提高了 4.8%。然而，它对域迁移和注意力模块的依赖带来了复杂性。另一种方法，DifferNet [220] 仅从正常样本中学习，但在大缺陷上受到噪声定位的影响。曹等人。 [221]提出了一种具有异常感知特征增强的知识蒸馏方法，尽管

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_027_1] EN: it depends heavily on the quality of the teacher model. Lastly, Shi et al. [222] proposes a lightweight DeepLabv3+ and MobileNetV2- based model enhanced with dynamic thresholding and cross-pseudo supervision, achieving over 86% mIoU, face limitations in adverse conditions (e.g., motion blur).  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_027_1] 中文：这在很大程度上取决于教师模型的质量。最后，石等人。 [222]提出了一种基于 DeepLabv3+ 和 MobileNetV2 的轻量级模型，通过动态阈值和交叉伪监督进行增强，实现了超过 86% 的 mIoU，在不利条件下（例如运动模糊）面临限制。

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_028_1] EN: 5.4.4. Self-supervised learning  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_028_1] 中文：5.4.4.自监督学习

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_029_1] EN: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is increasingly recognised as a robust method for industrial defect detection, particularly where labelled data is scarce. SSL enables models to learn from unlabelled data via pretext tasks, such as contrastive learning which constructs a feature space by using similar instances and repelling dissimilar ones using techniques such as SimCLR [223] with strong data augmentations and encoders, such as ResNet and ConvNet. One of the foundational methods, CutPaste by Li et al. [224], introduces synthetic anomaly generation through patch replacement, enabling robust training on only normal samples and achieving 95.2% AUC on MVTec AD. However, the synthetic patches may not fully mimic real defect patterns. Building upon this, Lv et al. [225] enhances spatial awareness using ECA-Net and CAM-loss, boosting AUC to 0.946, although manual tuning is required.  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_029_1] 中文：自监督学习 (SSL) 越来越被认为是一种可靠的工业缺陷检测方法，特别是在标记数据稀缺的情况下。 SSL 使模型能够通过借口任务从未标记的数据中学习，例如对比学习，它通过使用相似的实例构建特征空间，并使用 SimCLR [223] 等技术以及强大的数据增强和编码器（例如 ResNet 和 ConvNet）排斥不相似的实例。 Li 等人的 CutPaste 是基本方法之一。 [224]，通过图像块替换引入了合成异常生成，从而能够仅对正常样本进行稳健训练，并在 MVTec AD 上实现 95.2% AUC。然而，合成图像块可能无法完全模仿真实的缺陷模式。在此基础上，Lv 等人。 [225]使用 ECA-Net 和 CAM-loss 增强空间感知，将 AUC 提高到 0.946，尽管需要手动调整。

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_030_1] EN: Table 18 presents a summary of the papers in the field of SSL networks  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_030_1] 中文：表 18 列出了 SSL 网络领域的论文摘要

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_031_1] EN: for defect detection. For example, Zabin et al. [226] uses SimCLR framework with lightweight encoders, attaining 97.78% accuracy on NEU while reducing computational complexity, however, sensitive to augmentations and batch sizes. In other works, Hu et al. [227] applies SimSiam and earth mover’s distance for multi-target detection, and Wu et al. [228] fuses contrastive and inpainting objectives, achieving strong generalisation across five datasets. Despite its robustness, it suffers from performance drops on small defects and handcrafted pretext tasks. Xu et al. [229] designs SEDD, a lightweight segmentation model with homographic pseudo-labelling and spatial-channel attention, delivering 98.4% AUC, suitable for real-time applications but limited under texture-rich scenarios. Wang et al. [230] deploys advanced few-shot learning via TSMMIS, a self-supervised teacher–student framework using min–max instance similarity, achieving 95.5% accuracy on NEU but remaining sensitive to network depth and hyperparameter settings. Huang et al. [231] proposes ACViT-SCL, integrating metalearning with SimCLR and adaptive cross-transformers, performing well in low-shot regimes.  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_031_1] 中文：用于缺陷检测。例如，扎宾等人。 [226]使用带有轻量级编码器的 SimCLR 框架，在 NEU 上获得 97.78% 的准确率，同时降低计算复杂性，但对增强和批量大小敏感。在其他作品中，胡等人。 [227]应用SimSiam和推土机距离进行多目标检测，Wu等人。 [228]融合了对比和修复目标，在五个数据集上实现了强大的泛化。尽管它很稳健，但它在小缺陷和手工借口任务上的性能下降。徐等人。 [229]设计了SEDD，一种具有单应伪标签和空间通道注意力的轻量级分割模型，提供98.4％的AUC，适合实时应用，但在纹理丰富的场景下受到限制。王等人。 [230]通过 TSMMIS 部署先进的小样本学习，TSMMIS 是一个使用最小-最大实例相似性的自监督师生框架，在 NEU 上实现了 95.5% 的准确率，但对网络深度和超参数设置保持敏感。黄等人。 [231]提出了 ACViT-SCL，将元学习与 SimCLR 和自适应交叉Transformer相结合，在低样本情况下表现良好。

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_032_1] EN: Although SSL enhances representation learning in label-scarce settings, challenges remain in model calibration, sensitivity to augmentations, and real-world deployment efficiency. Future work may benefit from hybrid SSL-few-shot-transformer approaches to balance generalisability, performance, and real-time feasibility.  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_032_1] 中文：尽管 SSL 增强了标签稀缺环境中的表示学习，但模型校准、增强敏感性和实际部署效率方面仍然存在挑战。未来的工作可能会受益于混合 SSL-few-shot-transformer 方法来平衡通用性、性能和实时可行性。

[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_033_1] EN: 5.4.5. Weekly-supervised learning  
[PDF 第 17 页 | B017_033_1] 中文：5.4.5。弱监督（原文写作 Weekly-supervised）学习

[232] presents a WSL model by utilising image-level annotations, a siamese network is utilised to close the gap between image-level and pixel-level supervision. To improve performance without increasing computing complexity, the

[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_057_1] 中文：MIOU:最高86.32%的分区

[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_058_1] EN: approach made use of log summation exponential global pooling modules and max pooling-based non local attention. Xu et al. [233] uses VGG-based CNN with residual attention to detect defects in KolektorSDD using minimal labels. Luo et al. [234] proposed a memory-attended saliency framework combining FPN and attention, achieving strong results across DAGM and Severstal datasets. DefectGAN [235], a CycleGAN-based approach, achieved 81.05% IoU without pixel-level ground truth. Huang et al. [236] introduces an attention-enhanced nested U-Net++ reaching 100% AUC, though limited by complexity and synthetic data reliance. While WSL models reduce annotation costs and adapt well to various defects, their performance can be hindered by generalisability, deployment readiness, and reliance on synthetic or adversarial components.  
[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_058_1] 中文：Xu等人[233]使用基于VGG的有线电视新闻网,并留意利用最起码的标签来探测KolektorSDD中的缺陷。Loo等人[234] 提议建立一个将FPN和注意力结合起来的内存突出框架,在整个DGM和Severstal数据集中取得强有力的结果。 黄氏等人[236]引入了一个引人注意的嵌套式U-Net++,尽管受复杂性和合成数据依赖性的限制,达到100%的AUC。 尽管WSL模型降低了批注成本并适应了各种缺陷,但其性能可能因可概括性、部署准备状态和对合成或对抗成分的依赖而受到阻碍。

[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_059_1] EN: 5.4.6. Discussion  
[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_059_1] 中文：5.4.6. 讨论

[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_060_1] EN: In summary, compared with pyramid networks, attention based networks have advantages in defect information acquisition. However, for supervised techniques, similar to segmentation networks, detection networks also demand a substantial amount of labelled data, with pixel-level annotations. This often requires a significant effort and cost for labelling. However, defect detection methods based on semi-supervised and self-supervised methods are relatively more flexible and can take advantage of limited or incomplete data labelling. Therefore, in practical applications, an appropriate detection method can be selected according to the specific data situation and resource constraints.  
[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_060_1] 中文：总而言之,与金字塔网络相比,以关注为基础的网络在获取缺陷信息方面具有优势,但是,对于监督技术(类似于分割网络)来说,检测网络也需要大量贴有像素级说明的数据,这往往需要大量的努力和标签成本;然而,基于半监督和自监督方法的缺陷检测方法相对灵活,可以利用有限的或不完整的数据标签;因此,在实际应用中,可以根据具体数据状况和资源限制选择适当的检测方法。

[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_061_1] EN: 6. Other networks  
[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_061_1] 中文：6. 其他网络

[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_062_1] EN: Aside from YOLO-based models with feature pyramids and attention mechanisms, other models based on transformer architectures, LLM and foundation models are also used for defect detection and segmentation.  
[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_062_1] 中文：除了基于YOLO的模型外,还有地貌金字塔和注意力机制,其他基于Transformer结构、LLM和基础模型的模型也用于缺陷检测和分解。

[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_063_1] EN: 6.1. Transformer-based models  
[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_063_1] 中文：6.1. 以Transformer为基础的模型

[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_064_1] EN: Transformer-based models have revolutionised industrial defect detection by effectively capturing long-range dependencies and global contextual features. After the development of attention mechanisms, transformer-based models such as Vision Transformer (ViT), Swin Transformer, and DETR variants have demonstrated strong performance in segmentation, classification, and detection tasks, especially in steel, PCB, and textile defect inspection [238–240]. Table 20 presents a summary of the papers used transformer-based network for defect detection. Su et al. [238] introduces RT-DETR with CBAM for printed fabrics, while MS-DETR [240] achieves high performance in real-time PCB defect detection. Models such as RTCN [241], LSwin [243], CGTD-Net [245], and LMViT [247] further pushed boundaries in steel defect segmentation and classification. Chen et al. [258] suggests a model named EEE-Net which is an effective edge enhancement network that also uses transformer blocks and sequence reduction to improve detection accuracy, it incorporates an edge encoder, decoder and edge information fusion module. Experimental results on glass and mobile phone screen demonstrate the model’s superior performance and suitability for real-time industrial applications.  
[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_064_1] 中文：以变换器为基础的模型通过有效捕捉长距离依赖性和全球背景特征,使工业缺陷检测发生了革命性的变化。 在开发了注意力机制之后,基于变异器的模型(如愿景转换器、Swin变换器和DETR变量)在分割、分类和检测任务方面表现良好,特别是在钢材、多氯联苯和纺织品缺陷检查[238-240]。 表20提供了用于缺陷检测的变异器网络文件摘要。 Su 等人[238] 介绍了用于印刷布料的RT-DETR和CBAM,而MS-DETR [240] 在实时多氯联苯缺陷检测中取得了很高的性能,例如RTCN [241]、LSwin [243]、CGTD-Net [245] 和 LMViT[247] 在钢缺陷分解和分类方面进一步拉动了边界。Chen等人[258] 提出了一个名为EEE-Net的模型,这是一个有效的边缘增强网络,它也使用Transformer块和序列减少来提高探测精确度,其中包括一个边缘编码器、脱coder和边缘信息聚合模块。 玻璃和移动电话屏幕的实验结果显示,该模型的性能优异,适合实时工业应用。

[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_065_1] EN: Gao et al. [259] also implements a framework for surface defect detection utilising the transformer architecture. Their method employs patch embedding in the VSwin transformer to divide the input image into overlapping patches. These patches are able to extract more abstract data from the original input pixels. Afterwards, features from several patches are taken out and used to defect detection modules. When trained on small-scale datasets, their methodology outperforms transfer learning techniques. However, these models often face practical limitations including high memory usage, long inference times, and the need for large annotated datasets, making real-time deployment difficult. Su et al. [242] implements a model named PMSA-DyTr based on semantic aligners, dynamic encoder provides accurate results, rely on intricate modules that increase system complexity and  
[PDF 第 18 页 | B018_065_1] 中文：Gao等人[259] 也实施了一个使用Transformer结构的表面缺陷检测框架。 他们的方法是在 VSwin Transformer中嵌入图像块, 将输入图像分割为重叠的图像块。 这些图像块能够从原始输入像素中提取更多抽象的数据 。 随后, 从几个图像块中取出特征并用于解析检测模块。 当他们接受小规模数据集训练时, 其方法优于传输学习技术 。 然而,这些模型往往面临实际限制,包括内存使用率高、推断时间长以及需要大量附加说明的数据集,因此难以实时部署。 Su等人[242]采用一个以语义匹配器为基础的名为PMSA-DyTr的模型,动态编码器提供准确的结果,依赖复杂的模块来增加系统复杂性,

[PDF 第 19 页 | B019_053_1] 中文：IoU：81.05% 分割

[PDF 第 19 页 | B019_054_1] EN: over-detection risks. Likewise, JDCBL [248] incorporates a contextual transformer with contrastive loss for fabric inspection, but suffers from slow inference and manual tuning. To overcome these constraints, few-shot learning methods have been explored as lightweight, data-efficient alternatives. MAPTNet [239] and CPANet [249] show strong segmentation capabilities on small datasets, while EidNet [250] and DSRF [252] are tailored for classification and detection in low-sample environments. MFANet [251] effectively bridges coarse and fine defect granularity but can be computationally intensive. Although few-shot models reduce reliance on labelled data, they sometimes struggle with subtle or ambiguous defects. Ultimately, integrating transformer architectures with few-shot or self-supervised learning represents a promising path for building robust, scalable, and generalisable defect detection systems that suit the diverse demands of real-world industrial applications.  
[PDF 第 19 页 | B019_054_1] 中文：过度检测风险。同样，JDCBL [248] 结合了一个具有对比损失的上下文转换器来进行织物检查，但受到缓慢的推理和手动调整的影响。为了克服这些限制，人们已经探索了小样本学习方法作为轻量级、数据高效的替代方案。 MAPTNet [239] 和 CPANet [249] 在小数据集上显示出强大的分割能力，而 EidNet [250] 和 DSRF [252] 是为低样本环境中的分类和检测而定制的。 MFANet [251] 有效地连接了粗粒度和细粒度的缺陷，但计算量可能很大。尽管少样本模型减少了对标记数据的依赖，但它们有时会遇到微妙或模糊的缺陷。最终，将Transformer架构与少量学习或自监督学习相集成，为构建强大的、可扩展的和通用的缺陷检测系统提供了一条有前途的道路，以满足现实世界工业应用的不同需求。

[PDF 第 19 页 | B019_055_1] EN: Foundation models (FMs), including SAM, CLIP, and GPT, and Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as promising tools for industrial defect detection due to their strong generalisation capabilities and ability to operate in zero and few-shot learning scenarios [260]. These models are pretrained on extensive datasets and can be fine-tuned for specific tasks with minimal labelled data, making them well-suited for data-scarce environments. Table 21 presents a summary of the papers in this field for defect detection. For example, Jiang et al. [254] develops PA-SAM, an improved variant of SAM for efficient fine-tuning, and another module (e.g., IPEG) for automated prompt generation, achieving notable improvements on datasets, such as SD- Saliency-900 and Tire-Seg. LLMs are also gaining attraction for their ability to integrate vision-language reasoning and support multi-modal interactions. Zhang et al. [255] leverages ResNet-18 and fuzzy inference with LLMs to generate interpretable defect explanations. Similarly, AnomalyGPT [256] enables open-set detection and multi-turn dialogue  
[PDF 第 19 页 | B019_055_1] 中文：基础模型 (FM)，包括 SAM、CLIP 和 GPT，以及大型语言模型 (LLM)，由于其强大的泛化能力以及在零和少样本学习场景中运行的能力，已成为工业缺陷检测的有前途的工具 [260]。这些模型在广泛的数据集上进行了预训练，并且可以使用最少的标记数据针对特定任务进行微调，从而非常适合数据稀缺的环境。表 21 总结了该缺陷检测领域的论文。例如，江等人。 [254]开发了 PA-SAM，一种用于高效微调的 SAM 改进变体，以及另一个用于自动提示生成的模块（例如 IPEG），在数据集（例如 SD-Saliency-900 和 Tire-Seg）上实现了显着改进。法学硕士还因其整合视觉语言推理和支持多模式交互的能力而受到关注。张等人。 [255]利用 ResNet-18 和 LLM 的模糊推理来生成可解释的缺陷解释。类似地，AnomalyGPT [256] 实现了开集检测和多轮对话

[PDF 第 19 页 | B019_056_1] EN: for defect inspection, while Boonmee et al. [257] utilises GPT-4 for diagnosing industrial faults through iterative prompting. Despite their strengths, FMs often demand substantial memory and computational resources, and they struggle to adapt flexibly to domain-specific features without further tuning.  
[PDF 第 19 页 | B019_056_1] 中文：用于缺陷检查，而 Boonmee 等人。 [257]利用GPT-4通过迭代提示来诊断工业故障。尽管 FM 具有优势，但它们通常需要大量内存和计算资源，并且它们很难在不进一步调整的情况下灵活适应特定领域的功能。

[PDF 第 19 页 | B019_057_1] EN: 7. Highlights of the study

[PDF 第 19 页 | B019_058_1] EN: The methodology and accuracy of the studying research papers on DL-based methods for defect detection in industrial applications are described in the preceding section. This section outlines the statistical analysis of the defect detection techniques. According to the taxonomy described in Fig. 1, the entire literature is broadly categorised into two groups: semi-automated and automated defect detection methods.

From 2017 onward, there is a clear indication in the adoption of CNN and transfer learning, reflecting the field’s shift towards data-driven approaches. Around 2019, encoder–decoder architectures started gaining popularity, primarily for segmentation tasks. Attention-based and pyramid networks also have a gradual rise, indicating a growing focus on enhancing feature extraction and multi-scale representation. More recently, GAN, transformer-based architectures, and semi/self/weakly supervised learning methods have gained momentum. This trend highlights a movement towards models that can either generate synthetic data, work with limited labelled samples or handle more complex visual patterns. The growing trend of these modern techniques reflects continuous innovation aimed at improving the efficiency and accuracy of defect detection in various industrial sectors.

[PDF 第 21 页 | B021_014_1] EN: 8. Open challenges and future research directions  
[PDF 第 21 页 | B021_014_1] 中文：8. 开放挑战和未来研究方向

[PDF 第 21 页 | B021_015_1] EN: Automated inspection systems are becoming more and more crucial to quality assessment in the industry sector. Some researchers published review articles [27–30] on defect detection and classification in industrial products, but there are still many challenges left to tackle in this domain. This survey provides a comprehensive study of defect detection and classification methodology using image processing, ML and DL methods and describes how they are applied to the different products. This section presents the identified open challenges that are categorised as follows: (1) data pre-processing, (2) deep learning model, and (3) model reliability and trustworthiness. Fig. 14 represents a schematic diagram of future research direction visualising all critical components where challenges arise in the model for defect detection. In addition, some findings are investigated that should be considered in the future.  
[PDF 第 21 页 | B021_015_1] 中文：自动化检测系统对于工业领域的质量评估变得越来越重要。一些研究人员发表了关于工业产品缺陷检测和分类的评论文章[27-30]，但该领域仍然存在许多挑战需要解决。本次调查对使用图像处理、ML 和 DL 方法的缺陷检测和分类方法进行了全面研究，并描述了它们如何应用于不同的产品。本节介绍了已确定的开放挑战，分类如下：(1) 数据预处理，(2) 深度学习模型，(3) 模型可靠性和可信度。图 14 表示未来研究方向的示意图，将缺陷检测模型中出现挑战的所有关键组件可视化。此外，还研究了一些将来应考虑的发现。

[PDF 第 21 页 | B021_016_1] EN: 8.1. Data pre-processing in industrial settings  
[PDF 第 21 页 | B021_016_1] 中文：8.1.工业环境中的数据预处理

[PDF 第 21 页 | B021_017_1] EN: This section analyses the key challenges in collecting the dataset for defect inspection, as it is a necessary step for all DCNN models. Databases come with a series of challenges such as data cleaning, data labelling, data privacy and data sharing.  
[PDF 第 21 页 | B021_017_1] 中文：本节分析了收集缺陷检查数据集的主要挑战，因为这是所有 DCNN 模型的必要步骤。数据库带来了数据清洗、数据标签、数据隐私和数据共享等一系列挑战。

[PDF 第 21 页 | B021_018_1] EN: • Data Cleaning: The first challenge comes when collecting images from an industry, there are limitations in capturing the images such as non-uniform illumination and camera noise. Thus, this image can run into some issues, such as blurred images, defects that are not clearly visible or noise. So, this kind of image needs to be preprocessed before feeding them into the model. Cleaning the noise increases the data quality and makes the model more robust. Then data need to be resized as per model choice. • Data Labelling: The next challenge is data labelling, which is also crucial to the preparation of the dataset for the model, and therefore necessitates a lot of human work. Automated defect detection faces significant challenges due to data privacy, particularly in some domains where the industry keeps the data confidential. Moreover, some researchers do not like to share data publicly, which makes it difficult for other researchers to collect the dataset and train their models. Nahar et al. [261], develops a standardised data capturing policy for automated defect detection in trading cards. In the pre-processing stage, data undergoes noise reduction via a bilateral filter, ensuring clean inputs. Following this, an algorithm was implemented to crop portions of specific sizes from the cards, thereby establishing a database with uniform dimensions. In another research [229], a custom dataset for steel defect detection is created by systematically cropping data into predetermined patch sizes, each representing different defect types. However, in both cases, the datasets are initially labelled manually.  
[PDF 第 21 页 | B021_018_1] 中文：• 数据清理：第一个挑战来自于从行业收集图像时，捕获图像存在限制，例如照明不均匀和相机噪声。因此，该图像可能会遇到一些问题，例如图像模糊、缺陷不清晰可见或噪声。因此，这种图像在输入模型之前需要进行预处理。清除噪声可以提高数据质量并使模型更加稳健。然后需要根据模型选择调整数据大小。 • 数据标签：下一个挑战是数据标签，这对于模型数据集的准备也至关重要，因此需要大量的人力工作。由于数据隐私，自动缺陷检测面临着重大挑战，特别是在行业对数据保密的某些领域。此外，一些研究人员不喜欢公开共享数据，这使得其他研究人员很难收集数据集并训练他们的模型。纳哈尔等人。 [261]，开发了用于交易卡中自动缺陷检测的标准化数据捕获策略。在预处理阶段，数据通过双边滤波器进行降噪，确保输入干净。随后，实施算法从卡片中裁剪特定尺寸的部分，从而建立具有统一尺寸的数据库。在另一项研究[229]中，通过系统地将数据裁剪成预定的图像块大小来创建用于钢缺陷检测的自定义数据集，每个图像块大小代表不同的缺陷类型。然而，在这两种情况下，数据集最初都是手动标记的。

[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_002_1] EN: 139]. Defect localisation adds another layer of complexity, often addressed by specialised semantic networks such as Fully Convolutional Network [157] and U-Net [74]. However, these methods entail labourintensive pixel-wise annotation, prompting the development of unsupervised models (e.g.,GAN [264]) which alleviate the annotation burden at the expense of slightly lower accuracy compared to supervised approaches. These strategies collectively strive to optimise defect detection systems for real-world industrial applications.  
[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_002_1] 中文：变异性化增加了另一个复杂层次,通常由全演化网络[157]和U-Net[74]等专门语义学网络处理,但是,这些方法需要劳动密集型像素说明,促使开发无监督的模式(例如GAN[264]),从而减轻批注负担,而降低精确度略低于受监督的方法,这些战略共同致力于优化用于实际工业应用的缺陷探测系统。

[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_004_1] EN: This section highlights a great challenge in defect detection model. Although DL has emerged as a popular field for effective defect detection in various industries, recent works have pointed out that the outputs provided by these models are not well-calibrated, limiting their use in critical decision scenario. Another great Challenge in CNN’s network is explainability because they work as a black box, which is hard to understand how the decisions are being made. Moreover, after training a DL model, when it is applied to a real-world task, it is important to identify that no model is fault less, and there may be occurrences where its predictions may be uncertain. To refine and assess DL models, human expertise and knowledge are integrated into the DCNN. In this perspective, decisions can be made based on feedback from experts or by retraining the model. Henceforth, the scenario formulate some research questions.

[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_007_1] EN: This subsection describes the reliability of deep learning models in industrial defect detection, focusing on explainability, uncertainty estimation, and calibration. Together, these three aspects determine how trustworthy and robust a model is when deployed in real-time decision-making environments.  
[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_007_1] 中文：本小节描述了工业缺陷检测中深层学习模型的可靠性,侧重于可解释性、不确定性估计和校准。 这三个方面一起决定了在实时决策环境中部署模型时如何可信和可靠。

[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_008_1] EN: (a) Models reliability through explainable AI. This section delves into the challenges of decision-making and ensuring model reliability in real-time applications, particularly in CNNs. A major hurdle lies in the explainability of CNNs, given their black box nature, which makes understanding decision-making processes challenging. While CNN layers identify patterns used in decision-making, it is often unclear which patterns or features are prioritised and why. This lack of transparency poses significant concerns, especially in critical domains like medicine  
[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_008_1] 中文：(a) 通过可解释的大赦国际提供模型可靠性。本节探讨了决策方面的挑战,并确保实时应用中的模型可靠性,特别是在有线电视新闻网中。鉴于CNN的黑匣子性质,其解释性是一个重大障碍,这使得理解决策过程具有挑战性。尽管CNN层查明了决策中使用的模式,但往往不清楚哪些模式或特征被优先列出,为什么。这种缺乏透明度的现象引起了严重的关注,尤其是在医学等关键领域。

[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_009_1] EN: and autonomous vehicles, where understanding decision-making is paramount. To tackle this, researchers focus on Explainable AI (XAI), aiming to enhance reliability, trustworthiness, and transparency of AI systems. Various XAI methods, such as saliency map [265,266], Grad-cam explored to elucidate CNN decisions. While XAI methods enhance transparency, maintaining model confidence remains crucial for accurate decision-making. Other papers [267,268] also offers a review of XAI approaches in different applications.  
[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_009_1] 中文：为了解决这一问题,研究人员将注意力集中在可解释的AI(XAI)上,目的是提高AI系统的可靠性、可信度和透明度。 各种 XAI方法,如显性地图[265,266],Grad-cam 探索了阐明CNN决定的方法。 虽然XAI方法提高了透明度,但保持模范信任对于准确决策仍然至关重要。 其他文件[267,268] 也审查了 XAI在不同应用中的做法。

[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_010_1] EN: (b) Uncertainty estimation of DL-based models:. Another challenge in the performance evaluation of DL based models is uncertainty quantification (UQ) as existing defect detection methods are implemented based on clean, good and uncontaminated training data. The ability for UQ using current AI-based methods is not only limited in terms of computational resources but it also requires changes to topology and optimisation processes, as well as multiple performances to monitor model instabilities. To address this challenges Abbaszadeh Shahri et al. [269] proposes a novel solution, Automated Random Deactivating Connective Weights (ARDCW), without changing the optimisation processes. In a single feed-forward pass ARDCW can effectively evaluate the uncertainty and it does not require changes in the optimisation process, can be applied to already trained topology in a way that outperforms other models. Yu et al. [137] addresses image uncertainty due to ambient light and label uncertainty due to human errors in PCB surface inspection. The proposed learning model overcomes this limitation by using a symmetric residual filter and probabilistic classification. It is further improved by combining a joint probabilistic inference engine. These models performed well in both self-collected and public datasets. Their experimental result showed that it can detect defects in complex patterns also. Therefore, there are still challenges that need to be addressed. Future work in this research field should be taken into account by combining the models in such a way so that to yield a more lightweight model while ensuring high detection accuracy [146,270,271].  
[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_010_1] 中文：(b) 对基于DL模型的不确定性估计:基于DL模型的业绩评估面临的另一个挑战是不确定性量化(UQ),因为现有的缺陷检测方法是根据清洁、良好和未受污染的训练数据实施的。 为了应对这一挑战,Abbaszadeh Shahri等人[269]提出了一个新的解决方案,即自动随机断电连接重量(ARCW),但不改变优化过程;在单一的进料前传中,ARCW可以有效评估不确定性,而不需要对优化过程进行修改,从而能够以优于其他模型的方式应用于已经受过训练的地形学。 Yu等人[137]处理环境光线造成的图像不确定性和多氯联苯表面检查中人为错误造成的标签不确定性。 拟议的学习模式通过使用对称剩余过滤器和概率分类克服了这一限制,并通过结合联合概率推理引擎进一步改进了该模型。这些模型在自我采集和公共数据集方面都表现良好。其实验结果表明,它也可以发现复杂模式中的缺陷。因此,仍然存在一些需要应对的挑战。应当将各种模型合并起来,从而产生一个更轻重的模型,同时确保高探测准确性[146、270、271]。

[PDF 第 23 页 | B023_011_1] EN: (c) Calibration techniques for enhancing DNN confidence:. In the literature, it is shown that DNNs consistently shows excellent performance across a wide variety of tasks. However, they often suffer a lack of proper calibration. In real-time decision-making applications, such as quality inspection in industry, the confidence levels of deep networks are essential for instilling reliability in the system. To address this calibration challenge, various probabilistic and measurement-based methods have been proposed, including Temperature Scaling, Beta Calibration, Platt Scaling, Isotonic Regression, Matrix Scaling, and Vector Scaling [272–274]. These methods allow the calibration of DL models,

[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_002_1] EN: 8.3.2. RQ4: Integrating HITLA in DL-based models refinement  
[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_002_1] 中文：8.3.2. RQ4:将HITLA纳入基于DL的模型完善

[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_003_1] EN: After training a DL model, it is crucial to acknowledge that no model is perfect, and there may be instances where its predictions are uncertain. HITLA refines and assesses DL-based models outputs by integrating human expertise and knowledge [276,277]. In HITLA, the inclusion of humans in the learning process could be done at different levels depending on who was in control of the process. Active learning requires humans to annotate unlabelled samples, thereby expanding and improving the training dataset. Iterative retraining involves human input to refine the model based on newly labelled data, often using a pool-based scenario for querying instances. Confidence scoring prompts human intervention when predictions fall below a certain threshold, ensuring model accuracy and accountability [263,278,279]. Effective feedback collection, including identifying false positives and false negatives, aids in continuous model refinement [263]. Despite its effectiveness, challenges persist in addressing human time constraints and developing expert systems to handle flagged instances, highlighting ongoing concerns in industry applications.  
[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_003_1] 中文：训练深度学习模型后，重要的是要承认没有任何模型是完美的，并且在某些情况下其预测可能是不确定的。 HITLA 通过整合人类专业知识和知识来完善和评估基于 DL 的模型输出 [276,277]。在 HITLA 中，可以在不同级别上将人类纳入学习过程，具体取决于谁控制该过程。主动学习需要人类对未标记的样本进行标注，从而扩展和改进训练数据集。迭代再训练涉及人工输入，以根据新标记的数据完善模型，通常使用基于池的场景来查询实例。当预测低于某个阈值时，置信度评分会提示人为干预，从而确保模型的准确性和问责制[263,278,279]。有效的反馈收集，包括识别误报和漏报，有助于持续改进模型[263]。尽管其有效性，但在解决人类时间限制和开发专家系统来处理标记实例方面仍然存在挑战，这凸显了行业应用中持续存在的问题。

[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_004_1] EN: 8.3.3. Challenges in training time, inference, accuracy and continuous  
[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_004_1] 中文：8.3.3.训练时间、推理、准确性和连续性方面的挑战

[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_005_1] EN: monitoring One of the most critical challenges in deploying DL-based model for industrial defect detection lies in balancing inference speed with training time. While advanced models, such as modified YOLOv8 and DGNet demonstrate strong detection capabilities—achieving up to 98.36% mAP [187,188,205], they require substantial computational resources, limiting their use in low-latency settings such as edge devices or high-speed production lines. In some domains, such as fabric or steel inspection, inference times between 2–10 s may be acceptable; however, real-time applications demand much faster results. Lightweight models such as MobileNet-based architectures [156] offer faster inference but tend to under-perform on complex or low-contrast defects. Similarly, YOLO variants enhanced with attention or pyramid modules [182,186,203] improves accuracy but often increase latency due to added complexity. While inference time is often acceptable in many applications, training time becomes a critical bottleneck in systems designed for continuous monitoring and model updating. In production settings where new defect types emerge over time, frequent retraining is necessary, raising the question of how to adapt efficiently without compromising the model performance.  
[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_005_1] 中文：部署基于深度学习的工业缺陷检测模型的最关键挑战之一在于平衡推理速度与训练时间。虽然改进的 YOLOv8 和 DGNet 等高级模型表现出强大的检测能力，可实现高达 98.36% 的 mAP [187,188,205]，但它们需要大量的计算资源，限制了它们在边缘设备或高速生产线等低延迟设置中的使用。在某些领域，例如织物或钢材检查，2-10 秒之间的推理时间可能是可以接受的；然而，实时应用程序需要更快的结果。基于 MobileNet 的架构 [156] 等轻量级模型提供更快的推理速度，但在处理复杂或低对比度缺陷时往往表现不佳。类似地，通过注意力或金字塔模块增强的 YOLO 变体 [182,186,203] 提高了准确性，但由于复杂性增加，通常会增加延迟。虽然推理时间在许多应用中通常是可以接受的，但训练时间成为设计用于持续监控和模型更新的系统的关键瓶颈。在生产环境中，随着时间的推移会出现新的缺陷类型，因此需要频繁地进行重新训练，这就提出了如何在不影响模型性能的情况下有效适应的问题。

[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_006_1] EN: To address this, continuous monitoring and human feedback are essential for assessing how well the model performs on new data [276, 277]. HITLA systems allow uncertain or low-confidence predictions to be flagged for manual review, which not only supports quality control but also generates new labelled data for retraining. However, retraining strategies can vary depending on the data; some update the model incrementally with small datasets, while others wait to collect a large dataset before fine-tuning or retraining from scratch. Both approaches face trade-offs: smaller updates risk poor generalisation, while larger ones may suffer from data distribution shifts or increased training time. Moreover, fine-tuning via TL can also lead to catastrophic forgetting or reduced performance in previously well-performing classes for a small dataset [263]. As a result, ongoing research focuses on finding optimal strategies that balance training cost, update frequency, and performance retention. Incorporating efficient learning techniques such as TL, continuous learning, and adaptive fine-tuning is the key to maintaining robust and responsive models in real-world defect detection systems.  
[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_006_1] 中文：为了解决这个问题,持续监测和人类反馈对于评估该模型对新数据[276,277]的运行情况至关重要。 HITLA系统允许将不确定或低可信度预测标记为手工审查,这不仅支持质量控制,而且生成了新的标签数据供再训练使用。然而,再训练战略可以因数据而异;有些则以小数据集逐步更新模型,而另一些则等待从零开始微调或再训练前收集大量数据集。 这两种方法都面临权衡:小部分更新可能缺乏概括性,而较大部分则可能因数据分布变化或训练时间增加而受到影响。 此外,通过TL微调还可能导致灾难性地忘记或降低以前在小型数据集方面表现良好的类别的绩效[263]。因此,正在进行的研究侧重于找到平衡训练成本、更新频率和保持业绩的最佳战略。纳入高效学习技术,如TL、持续学习和适应性微调是维持现实世界缺陷检测系统强大和反应灵敏模型的关键。

[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_007_1] EN: 8.4. Future industry applications  
[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_007_1] 中文：8.4. 未来的工业应用

[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_008_1] EN: The complete study reveals that defect detection research is most prevalent in fabric (14–18 papers), steel (12–15 papers), and wood (10–12 papers), driven by industrial relevance and benchmark datasets, such as NEU-DET, DAGM, and TILDA. Moderate research (5–6 papers) exists for metal, ceramic, and glass, while domains such as concrete, plastic, battery, PCB, and engine/gears are less studied (2–3 papers each), often due to limited public datasets or proprietary data restrictions. Critically, only one paper focuses on paper/art paper and three on trading cards, highlighting them as severely underexplored [41,280– 282]. These areas lack benchmark datasets, hindering deep learning advancements. Their visual complexity and precision demands make them promising yet challenging frontiers for future research, particularly with targeted dataset creation and augmentation strategies. Currently, the whole process of detecting and classifying defects on trading cards relies on manual and subjective methods, which creates inconsistencies in quality assessment. Therefore, there is a promising chance to explore this field by addressing the challenges of defect detection on trading cards. To assess the quality of trading cards some criteria should be taken into account such as corner defects, edge defects, surface defects and centring measurement [263,283]. These defects are in responsible for assessing the overall quality and condition of the trading cards. Corner defects are irregularities or damages at card corners [263], affecting the size and physical appearance of the card while defects on the edge are imperfections along edges due to wear, or dent.  
[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_008_1] 中文：完整的研究表明,在工业相关性和基准数据集(如NEU-DET、DAGM和TILDA)的驱动下,在织物(14-18张纸)、钢材(12-15张纸)和木材(10-12张纸)中,缺陷检测研究最为普遍。 对金属、陶瓷和玻璃的研究(5-6张纸 ) , 而混凝土、塑料、电池、多氯联苯和发动机/齿轮(每张2-3张纸)则较少,这往往是由于公共数据集有限或专利数据限制所致。 关键地说,只有一篇论文侧重于纸/艺术纸张,而三篇论文则侧重于交易卡,强调它们被严重低估[41,280 - 282]。 这些领域缺乏基准数据集,阻碍了深层学习进步。 其视觉复杂性和精确性要求使它们为未来研究带来了充满挑战的前沿,特别是有目标的数据集创建和增强策略。 目前,对交易卡缺陷进行检测和分类的整个过程依赖于手动和主观方法,这在质量评估中造成了不一致。 因此,通过解决交易卡上的缺陷检测问题来探索这个领域是大有希望的机会。为了评估交易卡的质量,应当考虑一些标准,例如角缺陷、边缘缺陷、表面缺陷和中间测量[263,283],这些缺陷负责评估交易卡的总体质量和状况。

[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_008_2] EN: Surface defects include scratches, blemishes and markings, affecting visual appeal. By assessing all these defects one can evaluate trading card quality, aiding collectors. Alternately, widely researched area is defect detection on fabric [40,49,50,156]. Fabric inspection system plays a key role for quality assurance in textile manufacturing. There is an ever-growing demand in the textile factory to substitute the human intensive quality inspection performed with naked eyes by an automated inspection system. This task compelled researchers and practitioners to develop deep CNN-based approaches to isolate fabric defects.

[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_009_1] EN: 9. Conclusion  
[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_009_1] 中文：9. 结论

[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_010_1] EN: Recent advancements in AI and DL techniques have led to successful applications in industrial products for defect detection. Finding a suitable DL model for solving the defect detection problem is very difficult due to the particularities of industrial scenarios and different types of materials. This article provides a comprehensive study of defect detection and classification methodologies by studying more than 150 scientific contributions. The literature review verified that, due to the challenges associated with dataset labelling and annotation, scholars examine unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised and weeklysupervised models in addition to supervised models. Achieving the expected significant detection accuracy and training the model have issues associated with both types of models. Moreover, finding the defective region in a complex pattern poses another type of challenge.  
[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_010_1] 中文：人工智能和深度学习技术的最新进展已成功应用于工业产品的缺陷检测。由于工业场景和不同类型材料的特殊性，寻找合适的深度学习模型来解决缺陷检测问题非常困难。本文通过研究 150 多项科学贡献，对缺陷检测和分类方法进行了全面研究。文献综述证实，由于数据集标记和标注相关的挑战，除了监督模型之外，学者们还研究了无监督、自监督、半监督和弱监督（原文写作 Weekly-supervised）模型。实现预期的显着检测精度和训练模型都存在与这两种类型的模型相关的问题。此外，在复杂图案中找到缺陷区域也带来了另一种挑战。

[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_011_1] EN: Henceforth, this survey provides a comprehensive review of current research, firstly it describes the use of three approaches (e.g., manual, semi-automated and automated) in defect detection. It is found that the manual defect detection method can assist humans to identify defects but it completely depends on human judgement and needs a prior experimental setup. This problem can be solved by using semi-automated approaches but have some limitations. These methods are not capable of identifying different kinds of defects in different materials. ML and DL have gained popularity in defect detection as they can detect any kind of defect in every kind of material. In many cases, classical ML methods (semi-automated) have shown lower efficiencies than DL methods while the main challenge of DL techniques is depending on large training datasets. Secondly, this article explores the application of DL methods for defect detection on various materials, assessing their accuracy and performance. The DL-based models are  
[PDF 第 24 页 | B024_011_1] 中文：此后，本次调查对当前研究进行了全面回顾，首先描述了缺陷检测中三种方法（例如手动、半自动和自动）的使用。研究发现，手动缺陷检测方法可以帮助人类识别缺陷，但完全依赖于人类判断，并且需要事先进行实验设置。这个问题可以通过使用半自动化方法来解决，但有一些局限性。这些方法无法识别不同材料中的不同类型的缺陷。机器学习和深度学习在缺陷检测中广受欢迎，因为它们可以检测各种材料中的任何类型的缺陷。在许多情况下，经典的 ML 方法（半自动化）的效率低于 DL 方法，而 DL 技术的主要挑战取决于大型训练数据集。其次，本文探讨了深度学习方法在各种材料缺陷检测中的应用，评估其准确性和性能。基于深度学习的模型是

## PDF 第 25 页

[查看原 PDF 第 25 页](<AI-enabled defect detection in industrial products_ A comprehensive survey, key insights and future research challenges.pdf#page=25>)

[PDF 第 25 页 | B025_001_1] EN: discussed into several aspects: CNN-based, transfer learning and hybrid models for defect classification and encoder–decoder, pyramid networks, Yolo-based model and attention-based models for defect localisation. Our analysis found that encoder–decoder networks are the most prominent techniques in segmentation, while YOLO models with attention mechanisms dominated detection tasks. Additionally, unsupervised, semi-supervised, self-supervised and weekly-supervised models are also cover a good percentage of the overall literature. Furthermore, emerging approaches such as LLMs, foundation models, few-shot learning, and transformer-based methods have also shown increasing attention in recent literature. Finally, discovering the major challenges, it is anticipated that demand for automated inspection will continue to rise in both academic and industry enterprises.  
[PDF 第 25 页 | B025_001_1] 中文：我们的分析发现,编码器-编码器网络是最突出的分解技术,而注意力机制的YOLO模型则主导着检测任务。 此外,无监管、半监督、自监督和弱监督（原文写作 Weekly-supervised）的模型也覆盖了整个文献中的很好比例。 此外,新兴方法,如LLMS、基础模型、短视学习和Transformer法也在最近的文献中显示出日益受关注。 最后,发现主要的挑战后,预计对自动检查的需求在学术企业和产业企业都将继续增长。

[PDF 第 30 页 | B030_009_1] EN: Lutfun Nahar received her B.Sc. (Hons.) and M.S. degree in Computer Science and Engineering from the University of Chittagong, Bangladesh, in 2010 and 2011, respectively. She joined at International Islamic University Chittagong, Bangladesh in 2013 and worked as an assistant professor from 2017 until 2022. Currently, she is pursuing the Ph.D. degree with the deep object quality assessment at the School of Information and Communication Technology, Griffith University, Australia. Her current research interests are in the areas of machine learning, image processing and artificial intelligence.  
[PDF 第 30 页 | B030_009_1] 中文：Lutfun Nahar分别于2010年和2011年获得孟加拉国吉大港大学计算机科学和工程学博士学位(荣誉)和M.S.学位,2013年加入孟加拉国吉大港国际伊斯兰大学,从2017年至2022年担任助理教授。 目前,她正在澳大利亚格里菲思大学信息和通信技术学院攻读博士学位并进行深层次的物体质量评估。 她目前的研究兴趣在于机器学习、图像处理和人工智能领域。

[PDF 第 30 页 | B030_010_1] EN: Mohammad Awrangjeb is a Senior Lecturer at the School of Information and Communication Technology, Griffith University, Australia. He has completed his Ph.D. in Computer Science at the Monash University, Australia, in 2008. He received his M.S. degree in Computer Science and Engineering from the National University of Singapore (NUS), Singapore, in 2004 and B.Sc. (Engineering) in Computer Science from the Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology (BUET), in 2001. His research interest includes object extraction and modelling from remote sensing data, multimedia security and image processing, and fusion of remote sensing data.  
[PDF 第 30 页 | B030_010_1] 中文：Mohammad Awrangjeb是澳大利亚格里菲思大学信息和通信技术学院高级讲师,2008年在澳大利亚莫纳什大学完成计算机科学博士学位,2004年在新加坡国立大学获得计算机科学和工程硕士学位,2001年在孟加拉国工程和技术大学获得计算机科学理学士(工程师)。 他的研究兴趣包括利用遥感数据进行物体提取和建模、多媒体安全和图像处理以及遥感数据的聚合。
